### 📷 例 4701：城市旅行生活方式摄影

![城市旅行生活方式摄影](../../images/%E5%9F%8E%E5%B8%82%E6%97%85%E8%A1%8C%E7%94%9F%E6%B4%BB%E6%96%B9%E5%BC%8F%E6%91%84%E5%BD%B1.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
超写实生活方式摄影，主角为 {argument name="characters" default="两位时尚的年轻女性"}，捕捉她们在现代城市和文化地标中旅行与友谊的多个抓拍瞬间。其中一人留着浅棕色短波波头，带有柔和的刘海；另一人留着黑色长直发（或扎起发型的变体）。她们的面部特征在每张图像中保持一致。她们身着 {argument name="fashion style" default="潮流韩系/日系街头服饰"}，搭配廓形连帽衫、棒球衫、百褶裙、工装裤、牛仔单品、运动鞋、斜挎包、叠戴首饰、太阳镜和耳机。自然的微笑，放松的肢体语言，俏皮的互动，如比耶、比心、指向地标、并肩而坐或行走。场景包括：{argument name="locations" default="拥有高耸书架的现代书店、黄金时刻的韩国传统韩屋街道、背景中有山脉和历史宫殿的雨天城市人行横道，以及带有木质长椅的宁静绿色公园"}。采用 35mm 镜头，以平视和微仰视角拍摄，全身构图，电影级构图，浅景深，真实的叙事环境，柔和的自然日光，逼真的皮肤纹理，高度细腻的衣物面料，清晰的面部细节，明亮而自然的色彩分级，杂志级旅行摄影风格，社交媒体网红美学，超写实，单反画质，HDR，8K，清晰对焦，无畸变，无多余肢体，无重复人物，无文字，无水印。

[English]
Ultra-realistic lifestyle photography featuring {argument name="characters" default="two stylish young women"} captured in multiple candid travel and friendship moments across modern urban and cultural locations. One has a short light-brown bob haircut with soft bangs, while the other has long straight black hair (or tied-back variations). Their facial features remain consistent across every image. They wear {argument name="fashion style" default="trendy Korean/Japanese street fashion"} with oversized hoodies, varsity sweatshirts, pleated skirts, cargo pants, denim, sneakers, crossbody bags, layered jewelry, sunglasses, and headphones. Natural smiles, relaxed body language, playful interactions such as peace signs, heart hands, pointing toward landmarks, sitting together, and walking side by side. Scenes include: {argument name="locations" default="a contemporary bookstore with towering bookshelves, a traditional Korean hanok street at golden hour, a rainy city crosswalk with mountains and a historic palace in the background, and a peaceful green park with wooden benches"}. Shot at eye level and slightly low-angle perspectives using a 35mm lens, full-body framing, cinematic composition, shallow depth of field, authentic environmental storytelling, soft natural daylight, realistic skin texture, highly detailed clothing fabrics, crisp facial details, vibrant yet natural color grading, editorial travel photography, social media influencer aesthetic, ultra-photorealistic, DSLR quality, HDR, 8K, sharp focus, no distortion, no extra limbs, no duplicated people, no text, no watermark.
```

演播室产品摄影，{argument name="background" default="无缝浅灰色背景"}，柔和漫射光，构图完美居中，比例一致，正交参考风格，高细节织物纹理，防水材质，写实缝线，自然褶皱，精准比例，时尚目录级画质，照片级真实感，8K 分辨率，超清晰，无干扰阴影，专业服装设计展示。

The character wears a {argument name="details" default="green baseball cap, green crew-neck shirt, green hooded rain jacket with dark charcoal side panels"}, reflective silver strip near the hem, black slim-fit pants, and black athletic sneakers. White vertical branding printed on the jacket front panel and horizontal branding across the back. Neutral facial expression, athletic build, realistic skin texture.

### 🔧 例 4703：手绘黑色记号笔涂鸦

![手绘黑色记号笔涂鸦](../../images/%E6%89%8B%E7%BB%98%E9%BB%91%E8%89%B2%E8%AE%B0%E5%8F%B7%E7%AC%94%E6%B6%82%E9%B8%A6.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
请根据附图绘制一个简单的手绘涂鸦，就像是用 {argument name="tool" default="黑色记号笔"} 亲手勾勒的一样。使用自然的记号笔笔触、略显不稳的轮廓、细腻的手绘瑕疵以及微开的线条末端，营造出一种温暖的编辑风格涂鸦美感。避免使用干净的矢量图形和过于精致的数字效果。保持插图简约、通透、扁平且低调。仅使用 {argument name="accent color" default="天蓝色"} 作为点缀色。

[English]
Draw a simple hand-drawn doodle of the attached photo, as if it were sketched by a person using a {argument name="tool" default="black marker"}. Use natural marker strokes, slightly wobbly outlines, subtle hand-drawn imperfections, slightly open line endings, and create a warm editorial doodle aesthetic. Avoid clean vector-style graphics and overly polished digital perfection. Keep the illustration simple, airy, flat, and understated. Use {argument name="accent color" default="sky blue"} only as an accent color.
```

### 📷 例 4704：超现实垂直街道眩晕感

![超现实垂直街道眩晕感](../../images/%E8%B6%85%E7%8E%B0%E5%AE%9E%E5%9E%82%E7%9B%B4%E8%A1%97%E9%81%93%E7%9C%A9%E6%99%95%E6%84%9F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
一张超现实电影感照片，画面中一位 {argument name="person" default="孤独的年轻人"} 站在一条陡峭得不可思议的郊区街道中央，背对镜头，仿佛整个社区像巨大的悬崖一样在他脚下垂直坠落。道路戏剧性地向下延伸，通向一片广阔的 {argument name="setting" default="美式住宅"} 社区，那里布满了独栋房屋、修剪整齐的草坪、车道、停放的汽车、游泳池、树木、人行道和蓝色回收箱。极端的强制透视与高空无人机视角相结合，营造出一种令人眩晕的失重感和不可思议的尺度感。男子穿着简单的 {argument name="outfit" default="深蓝色 T 恤、深色长裤和浅色运动鞋"}，平静地站在超现实下坡的边缘。明亮的午间自然光，清晰的阴影，写实的建筑，超精细纹理，照片级高空摄影，电影级构图，令人费解的视觉错觉，梦幻般却又真实可信，深景深，焦点清晰，色彩逼真，垂直 1:1 构图，无文字，无水印。

[English]
A surreal cinematic photograph of a {argument name="subject" default="lone young man"} standing in the middle of an impossibly steep suburban street, viewed from behind, as if the entire neighborhood drops vertically beneath his feet like a giant cliff. The road stretches dramatically downward into a sprawling {argument name="neighborhood style" default="American residential"} neighborhood filled with detached houses, manicured lawns, driveways, parked cars, swimming pools, trees, sidewalks, and blue recycling bins. Extreme forced perspective combined with a high altitude aerial drone view creates a dizzying sense of vertigo and impossible scale. The man wears a simple {argument name="clothing" default="dark navy T shirt, dark pants, and light sneakers"}, standing calmly at the edge of the surreal descent. Bright natural midday sunlight, crisp shadows, realistic architecture, ultra detailed textures, photorealistic aerial photography, cinematic composition, mind bending optical illusion, dreamlike but believable, deep depth of field, sharp focus, realistic colors, vertical 1:1 framing, no text, no watermark.
```

### 📷 例 4705：日落沙漠肖像

![日落沙漠肖像](../../images/%E6%97%A5%E8%90%BD%E6%B2%99%E6%BC%A0%E8%82%96%E5%83%8F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
一位 {argument name="subject" default="年轻韩国女性"} 穿着一件 {argument name="outfit" default="赤陶色泳装，搭配暖铜色面纱"}，面纱不对称地披在单肩上。黄金时刻的逆光在她的轮廓边缘勾勒出温暖的橙色光晕，近景侧面特写，平视视角，脸颊和颈部带有柔和的暖光，背景是模糊的沙漠地平线。竖构图肖像，2:3 宽高比。渲染要求：具备摄影级写实感，阴影区域带有细微的 ISO 400 胶片颗粒，避免过度平滑的 CG 质感。皮肤：呈现次表面散射和微纹理。头发：保留细微的自然瑕疵。

[English]
A {argument name="subject" default="young Korean woman"} wears a {argument name="outfit" default="terracotta swimsuit with a warm copper veil"} draped asymmetrically across one shoulder. Golden hour backlight rims her silhouette in warm orange, close-up profile shot, eye level, soft warm glow on her cheek and neck, desert horizon glowing dimly out of focus behind her. Vertical portrait orientation, 2:3 aspect ratio. Render with photographic realism: subtle ISO 400 film grain in shadow areas, avoid over-smoothed CG textures. Skin: subsurface scattering and micro-texture. Hair: subtle natural imperfection.
```

### 📷 例 4706：奢华顶层公寓镜面自拍

![奢华顶层公寓镜面自拍](../../images/%E5%A5%A2%E5%8D%8E%E9%A1%B6%E5%B1%82%E5%85%AC%E5%AF%93%E9%95%9C%E9%9D%A2%E8%87%AA%E6%8B%8D.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
在日落后的豪华酒店套房内拍摄的超写实镜面自拍，背景为拥有全景玻璃墙的优雅顶层公寓，俯瞰着璀璨的都市天际线。巨大的落地窗形成了发光摩天大楼、绚丽城市灯光与柔和夜间光斑的层叠倒影，与精致的室内设计完美融合。一位美丽的年轻女性留着 {argument name="hair color" default="栗棕色"} 的长发，发丝呈现出自然光泽的波浪卷，随意地坐在现代 {argument name="furniture" default="奶油色真皮躺椅"} 的边缘，身体微微侧向一边，对着镜子露出轻松自然的微笑。她单手在肩部高度拿着一部哑光黑色智能手机，自然地遮住部分脸庞，呈现出地道的社交媒体风格自拍。她身穿一件 {argument name="outfit" default="丝滑象牙金缎面中长裙"}，细肩带设计，垂坠感极佳，奢华的面料在温暖的环境光下泛着细腻的珍珠光泽。
**这间高档套房拥有丰富的橡木装饰、现代设计师家具、质感中性色调的装饰，以及一张光滑的大理石边桌，桌上摆放着水晶水杯、设计师手拿包、香薰蜡烛和一本奢华的咖啡桌读物。嵌入式天花板灯具和优雅落地灯发出的温暖间接照明营造出私密氛围，窗外充满活力的天际线倒影与室内空间完美交融。电影级构图、写实智能手机摄影、柔和景深、高端旅行美学、时尚杂志质感、自然皮肤纹理、真实光影、高动态范围、超细节写实、精致奢华生活方式、Instagram 风格构图，以旗舰智能手机拍摄，垂直 9:16 画幅。**

[English]
Ultra-realistic luxury hotel suite mirror selfie captured after sunset inside an elegant penthouse with panoramic glass walls overlooking a dazzling metropolitan skyline. The expansive windows create layered reflections of illuminated skyscrapers, colorful city lights, and soft nighttime bokeh, blending seamlessly with the sophisticated interior. A beautiful young woman with long {argument name="hair color" default="chestnut-brown"} hair styled in loose, glossy waves sits casually on the edge of a modern {argument name="furniture" default="cream leather chaise lounge"}, her body angled slightly away while looking toward the mirror with a relaxed, effortless smile. She holds a matte black smartphone in one hand at shoulder height, naturally covering part of her face in an authentic social-media-style selfie. She wears a {argument name="outfit" default="silky ivory-gold satin midi dress"} with slender shoulder straps and a fluid drape, the luxurious fabric reflecting the warm ambient lighting with a subtle pearlescent glow.
**The upscale suite features rich oak wood accents, contemporary designer furnishings, textured neutral décor, and a sleek marble side table topped with a crystal water glass, a designer clutch, a scented candle, and a luxury coffee-table book. Warm indirect lighting from recessed ceiling fixtures and elegant floor lamps creates an intimate atmosphere while the reflections of the vibrant skyline merge beautifully with the room's interior. Cinematic framing, realistic smartphone photography, soft depth of field, premium travel aesthetic, editorial fashion feel, natural skin texture, authentic lighting, high dynamic range, ultra-detailed realism, refined luxury lifestyle, Instagram-worthy composition, captured on a flagship smartphone in a vertical 9:16 aspect ratio.

### 🎬 例 4707：电影感 BMX 分镜脚本页

![电影感 BMX 分镜脚本页](../../images/%E7%94%B5%E5%BD%B1%E6%84%9F%20BMX%20%E5%88%86%E9%95%9C%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC%E9%A1%B5.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：为一段名为 {argument name="storyboard title" default="第一部分：觉醒——初次蹬踏"} 的短篇 AI 视频序列创建一份单色电影感分镜脚本页，展示一名年轻女性骑着 BMX 自行车穿过狭窄城市小巷的场景。

画布：横向分镜项目，比例约为 16:9，米白色纸张背景，细黑色边框，整洁的制作规划布局。使用灰度铅笔、水墨渲染，并带有轻微的胶片颗粒感，呈现细腻的漫画写实风格。左上角包含粗体大写标题；右上角包含小型标签“时长：15 秒”。

主视觉面板：页面上半部分为一个大型宽幅定场镜头。展示 {argument name="character name" default="JURI"}，一位年轻的东亚女性，留着黑色长直发，皮肤白皙，表情冷静自信，身穿宽松白色 T 恤、灰色阔腿裤和白色运动鞋，坐在黑色 BMX 自行车上。她转过头看向观众，双手握住车把，背景是一个安静、狭窄的韩式城市小巷，有百叶窗店面、混凝土墙、管道、涂鸦、小型标志以及强烈的线性透视。在右侧墙壁上添加一个小型韩国停车相关标志以增加真实感。

布局：在主视觉面板下方，创建 10 个带编号的分镜缩略图，排列为两行，每行五个。每个缩略图左上角有一个黑色小数字标签，下方有一个紧凑的制作表格，包含 5 个明确的行：“镜头 / 动作”、“摄像机 / 运动”、“时长”、“声音”和“转场”。保持字体微小但清晰可辨，如同专业导演的分镜脚本。

分镜缩略图，共 10 个面板：
1. 空巷的宽幅定场镜头；静态广角摄像机；1.5 秒；轻微城市环境音和柔和风声；切镜。
2. JURI 从后方骑 BMX 进入画面；进入时轻微平移；1.5 秒；脚步声和车轮滚动声；切镜。
3. 手部握住车把的特写；微距特写，浅景深；1.0 秒；握把调整和细微的链条转动声；切镜。
4. 脚踩踏板的特写；特写，轻微推镜头；1.0 秒；轮胎摩擦声和链条咔哒声；切镜。
5. 她蹬地起步；低位 3/4 角度；1.5 秒；蹬地和车轮滚动声；切镜。
6. 侧向跟拍镜头，自行车开始滚动；从左向右跟拍；1.5 秒；车轮滚动和链条转动声；切镜。
7. 前轮与路面的低角度镜头；低位跟拍特写；1.5 秒；轮胎滚动声和路面质感；切镜。
8. 她建立自信时的正面中景；缓慢推镜头；1.5 秒；车轮滚动声和轻微城市环境音；切镜。
9. 她向前骑行时阳光照在头发上；轻微跟拍，柔和背光；1.5 秒；风声和车轮滚动声；切镜。
10. 通往下一个地点的宽幅离开镜头；静态广角摄像机；1.5 秒；车轮滚动声渐弱融入城市环境音；转场“切至第二部分”。

底部信息栏：添加 4 个带简单黑色线条图标和标签的方框注释。1) 扬声器图标：“声音设计（第一部分）”，包含关于车轮滚动、脚步声、轮胎摩擦、链条转动、轻微城市环境音和柔和风声的说明。2) 定位图标：“场景 / 地点”，标注“首尔，安静小巷，白天”。3) 星形图标：“基调与氛围”，标注“青春、城市、自由、自信、极简、时尚广告风”。4) 剪贴板图标：“备注”，标注“保持自然的节奏和呼吸感。让 JURI 的存在感和动作引领整个场景”。

视觉风格：黑白分镜概念艺术，写实的铅笔线条，柔和的灰色水彩阴影，电影感韩国街道氛围，精确的面板边框，整洁的制作表设计。除灰度色调外避免使用彩色。无标志或水印。除小巷中零星的韩文标志外，所有文字均保持英文。

[English]
Goal: Create a monochrome cinematic storyboard sheet for a short AI-video sequence titled {argument name="storyboard title" default="PART 1. AWAKENING — THE FIRST PUSH"}, showing a young woman with a BMX bicycle beginning to ride through a narrow urban back alley.

Canvas: Landscape storyboard board, approximately 16:9, off-white paper background, thin black border, clean production-planning layout. Use grayscale pencil, ink wash, and subtle manga-realism rendering with light film grain. Top left contains the bold uppercase title; top right contains the small label “DURATION: 15 SECONDS”.

Main hero panel: A large wide establishing frame spanning the top half of the page. Show {argument name="character name" default="JURI"}, a youthful East Asian woman with long straight black hair, pale skin, calm confident expression, oversized white T-shirt, loose gray pants, and white sneakers, seated on a black BMX bike. She is turned over her shoulder looking toward the viewer while holding the handlebars in a quiet, narrow Korean-style city alley with shuttered storefronts, concrete walls, pipes, graffiti, small signs, and strong linear perspective. Add a small Korean parking-related sign on the right wall for authenticity.

Layout: Beneath the hero panel, create exactly 10 numbered storyboard thumbnails arranged in two rows of five. Each thumbnail has a small black number tab in the top-left corner and a compact production table underneath with exactly 5 labeled rows: “SHOT / ACTION”, “CAMERA / MOVEMENT”, “DURATION”, “SOUND”, and “TRANSITION”. Keep the typography tiny but legible, like a real director’s storyboard.

Storyboard thumbnails, exactly 10 panels:
1. Wide establishing shot of the empty alley; static wide camera; 1.5 sec; light city ambience and soft wind; cut.
2. JURI enters the frame with the BMX from behind; slight pan as she enters; 1.5 sec; footsteps and wheel roll; cut.
3. Close-up of her hand gripping the handlebar; macro close-up with shallow focus; 1.0 sec; grip shift and subtle chain movement; cut.
4. Close-up of her foot on the pedal; close-up, slight push-in; 1.0 sec; tire scrub and chain click; cut.
5. She pushes off to start moving; low 3/4 angle; 1.5 sec; foot push and wheel roll; cut.
6. Side tracking shot as the bike begins rolling; tracking left to right; 1.5 sec; wheel roll and chain movement; cut.
7. Low angle on front wheel and pavement; low tracking close-up; 1.5 sec; tire roll and pavement texture; cut.
8. Medium front shot as she gains confidence; slow push-in; 1.5 sec; wheel roll and slight city ambience; cut.
9. Sunlight catches her hair as she rides forward; slight tracking with soft backlight; 1.5 sec; wind and wheel roll; cut.
10. Wide exit frame leading into the next location; static wide camera; 1.5 sec; wheel roll fading into city ambience; transition “Cut to Part 2”.

Bottom information bar: Add exactly 4 boxed notes with simple black line icons and labels. 1) Speaker icon: “SOUND DESIGN (PART 1)” with notes about wheel roll, footsteps, tire scrub, chain movement, light city ambience, and soft wind. 2) Location pin icon: “SETTING / LOCATION” with “Seoul, quiet back alley, daytime.” 3) Star icon: “TONE & MOOD” with “Youthful, urban, free, confident, minimal, fashion-commercial.” 4) Clipboard icon: “NOTES” with “Maintain natural pacing and breath. Let JURI’s presence and movement lead the scene.”

Visual style: Black-and-white storyboard concept art, realistic pencil linework, soft gray watercolor shading, cinematic Korean street atmosphere, precise panel borders, clean production sheet design. Avoid color except grayscale tones. No logos or watermarks. Keep all text in English except small incidental Korean signage in the alley.

### 🎬 例 4709：零重力生存故事板

![零重力生存故事板](../../images/%E9%9B%B6%E9%87%8D%E5%8A%9B%E7%94%9F%E5%AD%98%E6%95%85%E4%BA%8B%E6%9D%BF.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：为一段 15 秒的硬核复古未来主义动画风格的零重力生存短片，创作一个详细的 16 格故事板。

画布：一张宽幅 4:3 的故事板，划分为 16 个相等的格子，呈 4 行 4 列排列，中间有细黑线分隔。在每个格子的左上角标注白色小数字，从 1 到 16 编号。全程使用粗粝的电影感光效、密集的线条、赛璐珞风格的动画渲染、划痕金属质感、青色冷却剂蒸汽、漂浮碎片以及红色应急灯光。

场景：废弃的轨道空间站 {argument name="station name" default="the Halcyon"}，一个受损的零重力圆柱形维护走廊。环境阴暗、工业感强、空间狭窄，布满了裸露的导管、破裂的面板、警示灯、火花、漂浮的螺栓、损坏的工具以及朦胧的青色冷却剂云雾。

角色：在每个相关格子中保持两名原始角色不变。角色一为 {argument name="young survivor" default="一名短发、橘色头发的年轻女性，身穿锈橘色压力服，佩戴机械护手，胸前有圆形仪表"}。角色二为 {argument name="veteran survivor" default="一名年长的深色皮肤男性，身穿破损的深蓝色装甲维护服，戴着头带式护目镜，留着胡茬，面容坚毅"}。两人都在零重力中漂浮，显得狼狈、急迫，虽恐惧但坚定。

逐格动作，共 16 格：
1. 俯瞰阴暗废弃走廊的广角镜头，青色冷却剂蒸汽在通道中翻涌，红色应急灯闪烁，碎片在零重力中漂浮。
2. 破裂数字警示监视器的特写，红色块状文字显示“00:14:87”和“HULL INTEGRITY FAILING”（船体完整性失效）。
3. 年轻幸存者在走廊中向前飞行的动态镜头，手臂向观众伸出，机械护手清晰可见，身后是青色蒸汽。
4. 老兵幸存者靠在墙板上，切割或焊接接缝处，迸发出明亮的橘色火花。
5. 广角镜头展示两名幸存者在走廊中分离，年轻幸存者向左漂浮，老兵在更后方，周围环绕着青色蒸汽和碎片。
6. 俯视/倾斜的零重力视角，展示损坏的空间站设备、管道、漂浮物以及远处的火花喷溅。
7. 年轻幸存者紧握金属管或扶手的动作特写，表情紧张，橘色防护服上有划痕，身后是红光和烟雾。
8. 老兵幸存者继续切割墙板的重复角度特写，明亮火花四溅，可见其破损的袖口和紧绷的表情。
9. 年轻幸存者漂过一张破裂的相框或反光板的侧面特写，映出两张面孔，暗示一段私人记忆；她神情专注且担忧。
10. 老兵幸存者穿过舱门或狭窄通道的正面特写，身后是红色警示灯，一只戴手套的手向观众抬起。
11. 从年轻幸存者身后拍摄的过肩动作镜头，她正向走廊另一侧的老兵幸存者伸出手。
12. 透过浓厚青色冷却剂云雾的广角镜头，两名幸存者在两侧隐约可见，碎片在他们之间翻滚。
13. 老兵幸存者在漂浮的碎片和蒸汽中绝望地向镜头伸手的特写，面部表情紧绷。
14. 两名幸存者在零重力中紧握戴手套双手的极度特写，背景是青色蒸汽和漂浮碎片。
15. 广角镜头展示两名幸存者正奋力向走廊尽头明亮的蓝白色逃生舱门游去，周围是红色应急灯和青色烟雾。
16. 圆形舱门框住的最终中景镜头：年轻幸存者和老兵幸存者并排漂向观众，精疲力竭但依然活着，周围环绕着清冷的蓝色舱门光线和红色的空间站灯光。

视觉风格：硬核复古未来主义动画故事板，高细节线条，粗粝的科幻生存氛围，动态漫画风格角度，戏剧性的透视缩短，浓重的阴影，青色与红色的配色方案，电影般的动感，拒绝现代 UI 的平滑感。

限制：必须使用 16 个编号格子，不得增加额外格子。保持两名角色、废弃空间站、青色冷却剂蒸汽、红色应急灯光以及服装在整个故事板中保持一致。避免使用对话气泡、说明文字、水印、标志或除格子编号及监视器文字之外的任何额外文字。

[English]
Goal: Create a detailed 16-panel storyboard for a 15-second animated zero-gravity survival short in a hard-edged retro-futurist anime style.

Canvas: One wide 4:3 storyboard sheet divided into exactly 16 equal panels in a 4-column by 4-row grid, with thin black gutters. Put a small white panel number in the top-left corner of each panel, numbered 1 through 16. Use gritty cinematic lighting, dense linework, cel-shaded anime rendering, scratched metal textures, teal coolant vapor, floating debris, and red emergency lights throughout.

Setting: The derelict orbital station {argument name="station name" default="the Halcyon"}, a damaged cylindrical maintenance corridor in zero gravity. The environment is dark, industrial, cramped, and full of exposed conduits, cracked panels, warning lights, sparks, drifting bolts, broken tools, and foggy teal coolant clouds.

Characters: Keep the same two original characters in every relevant panel. Character one is {argument name="young survivor" default="a short-haired orange-haired young woman in a rust-orange pressure suit with a mechanical gauntlet and a round chest gauge"}. Character two is {argument name="veteran survivor" default="an older dark-skinned man in a torn navy armored maintenance suit with a headband visor, stubble, and a rugged face"}. Both are floating in zero gravity, battered, urgent, and afraid but determined.

Panel-by-panel action, exactly 16 panels:
1. Wide establishing shot down the dark derelict corridor, teal coolant vapor billowing through the tunnel, red emergency lights glowing, debris drifting in zero gravity.
2. Close-up of a cracked digital warning monitor reading "00:14:87" and "HULL INTEGRITY FAILING" in red blocky text.
3. Dynamic shot of the young survivor flying forward through the corridor, arm outstretched toward the viewer, mechanical gauntlet visible, teal vapor behind her.
4. The veteran survivor braces himself against a wall panel and cuts or welds at a seam, producing bright orange sparks.
5. Wide shot showing both survivors separated in the corridor, the young survivor drifting left while the veteran is farther back, surrounded by teal vapor and debris.
6. Overhead/tilted zero-gravity view of broken station equipment, pipes, floating objects, and a burst of sparks in the distance.
7. Tight action close-up of the young survivor gripping a metal pipe or rail, face tense, orange suit scratched, red lights and smoke behind her.
8. Repeat-angle close shot of the veteran survivor still cutting the wall panel, shower of bright sparks, his torn sleeve and strained expression visible.
9. Side-profile close-up of the young survivor drifting past a cracked framed photograph or reflective panel showing two faces, suggesting a personal memory; she looks focused and worried.
10. Frontal close-up of the veteran survivor pushing forward through a hatchway or narrow passage, red warning lights behind him, one gloved hand raised toward the viewer.
11. Over-the-shoulder action shot from behind the young survivor as she reaches toward the veteran survivor across a corridor opening.
12. Wide shot through a dense teal coolant cloud with both survivors partially visible on opposite sides, debris tumbling between them.
13. Close-up of the veteran survivor reaching desperately toward camera through the floating debris and vapor, face strained.
14. Extreme close-up of the two survivors clasping gloved hands in zero gravity, centered against teal vapor and drifting debris.
15. Wide shot of both survivors pulling themselves toward a bright blue-white escape hatch or airlock at the end of the corridor, red emergency lights and teal smoke around them.
16. Final medium shot framed by a circular/rounded hatch: the young survivor and veteran survivor are side by side, floating toward the viewer, exhausted but alive, surrounded by cool blue hatch light and red station lights.

Visual style: Hard-edged retro-futurist anime storyboard, high-detail inked panels, gritty sci-fi survival mood, dynamic manga-style angles, dramatic foreshortening, heavy shadows, teal-and-red color palette, cinematic motion, no clean modern UI polish.

Constraints: Use exactly 16 numbered panels and no extra panels. Keep the same two characters, same derelict station, same teal coolant vapor, same red emergency lighting, and consistent costumes across the entire storyboard. Avoid speech bubbles, captions, watermarks, logos, or extra text except the panel numbers and the warning monitor text.
```

### 🏷️ 例 4711：复古未来主义零重力角色设定集

![复古未来主义零重力角色设定集](../../images/%E5%A4%8D%E5%8F%A4%E6%9C%AA%E6%9D%A5%E4%B8%BB%E4%B9%89%E9%9B%B6%E9%87%8D%E5%8A%9B%E8%A7%92%E8%89%B2%E8%AE%BE%E5%AE%9A%E9%9B%86.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：为一部 15 秒的零重力生存动画短片创作一张详尽、无文字的角色设定图，采用硬核复古未来主义动画风格。

画布：16:9 横向布局，背景为干净的深炭灰色，呈现专业动画制作设定集的质感。画面中不得出现任何标题、标签、Logo、说明文字、水印或 UI 文字。

角色：展示 2 名原创角色：{argument name="first character name" default="KES VOSS"} 和 {argument name="second character name" default="DARIUS"}。

视觉风格：粗粝的 1980 年代/1990 年代赛璐珞动画概念艺术，硬朗的墨线，低饱和度的工业色彩，磨损的织物，陈旧的金属，克制的高光，写实的比例，戏剧性且清晰的布光。氛围应体现出紧张、实用和生存导向，带有空间站的污垢感和复古模拟技术特征。

布局与元素统计：包含 16 个独立的视觉元素，排列成一张完整的角色设定拼贴图。左侧较年轻的角色包含 8 个元素：1 个身穿橙色压力服的全身正面图，1 个全身背面图，1 个四分之三侧面的胸部以上肖像，1 个低头且神情紧张的小型侧面肖像，1 个腕部模拟仪表和机械前臂支架的特写，1 个鞋底细节，1 个底部动作分镜（展示角色在零重力下焊接或切割，伴有明亮火花），以及 1 个底部特写分镜（展示角色在头盔或驾驶舱内专注的表情）。右侧较年长的角色包含 8 个元素：1 个身穿厚重蓝灰色生存服的全身正面图，1 个全身背面图，3 个头部研究（展示不同角度和严厉表情，留着白色短发，面部有皱纹），1 个带有悬挂金属环的领口或约束环配件，1 双厚重手套，以及 1 个底部动作分镜（展示他在零重力下修理设备，伴有明亮火花）。此外，在右下角整合 1 个道具小图：一台粗犷的复古便携式计算机/现场终端，带有破裂的绿色 CRT 屏幕和厚实的按钮；保持其与设定集的视觉连贯性，但不要添加任何文字。

角色细节：较年轻的角色是一位身材苗条、短发的年轻人，拥有 {argument name="hair color" default="锈橙色"} 头发，锐利的动画风格眼睛，严肃且警惕的表情，身穿橙色实用压力服，黑色内衬领口，系带背带，粗犷的靴子，手套，膝盖处有补丁，胸前佩戴圆形模拟仪表，通过带子和软管连接。一侧前臂应呈现机械化或加固状态，带有外露组件和绑带式压力表。

较年长的角色是一位身材魁梧、敦实的年长男性，深色皮肤，白色卷短发，留着白色胡茬，浓眉，疲惫而严厉的眼神，面容饱经风霜。他身穿蓝灰色厚重太空生存服，配有厚手套、靴子、腰带、背带、补丁、划痕，一只袖子有撕裂痕迹，部分头部研究中带有护目镜/护目镜带。

底部电影感长条：沿下边缘包含 4 个矩形故事分镜：1 个年轻角色在太空中焊接/切割，1 个年轻角色在头盔/驾驶舱内的特写，1 个年长角色修理设备并伴有火花，以及 1 个带有破裂屏幕终端的太空外部生存镜头。这些分镜应比主转面图更暗、更具电影感，包含星空、飞船残骸、电缆、火花和零重力感。

约束条件：不得出现任何可读文字、Logo、额外角色，不得使用可爱的 Q 版风格，不得使用明亮干净的科幻美学。背景保持平整的深炭灰色，画面构图紧凑但清晰易读。

[English]
Goal: Create a detailed, text-free character sheet for a 15-second animated zero-gravity survival short in a hard-edged retro-futurist anime style.

Canvas: 16:9 horizontal layout on a clean dark charcoal background, like a professional animation production sheet. No titles, labels, logos, captions, watermarks, or UI text anywhere.

Characters: Show exactly 2 original characters: {argument name="first character name" default="KES VOSS"} and {argument name="second character name" default="DARIUS"}.

Visual style: Gritty 1980s/1990s cel-shaded anime concept art, hard ink lines, muted industrial colors, scuffed fabric, worn metal, restrained highlights, realistic proportions, dramatic but readable lighting. The mood should feel tense, practical, and survival-focused, with space-station grime and retro analog technology.

Layout and counted elements: Include exactly 16 discrete visual elements arranged as a single character sheet collage. For the younger character on the left, include 8 elements: 1 full-body front view in an orange pressure suit, 1 full-body back view, 1 chest-up portrait facing three-quarter view, 1 smaller side-profile portrait looking down and tense, 1 close-up of a wrist-mounted analog gauge and mechanical forearm brace, 1 small boot-sole detail, 1 bottom action panel showing the character floating in zero gravity while cutting or welding with bright sparks, and 1 bottom close-up panel showing the character inside a helmet or cockpit with a focused expression. For the older character on the right, include 8 elements: 1 full-body front view in a bulky blue-gray survival suit, 1 full-body back view, 3 head studies showing different angles and stern expressions with short white hair and facial wrinkles, 1 collar or restraint ring accessory with a dangling metal ring, 1 pair of heavy gloves, and 1 bottom action panel showing him working on equipment in zero gravity with bright sparks. In addition, integrate 1 bottom-right prop vignette of a rugged retro portable computer/field terminal with a cracked green CRT screen and chunky buttons; keep it visually connected to the sheet but do not add text.

Subject details: The younger character is a slim, short-haired young adult with {argument name="hair color" default="rusty orange"} hair, sharp anime eyes, a serious guarded expression, an orange utilitarian pressure suit, black undersuit collar, harness straps, rugged boots, gloves, patched knees, and a round analog chest gauge connected by straps and hoses. One forearm should appear mechanical or reinforced with exposed components and a strapped pressure dial.

The older character is a broad, stocky elderly man with dark skin, short white curly hair, a white mustache and beard stubble, heavy brow, tired stern eyes, and a weathered face. He wears a blue-gray bulky space survival suit with thick gloves, boots, belt, suspenders, patches, scuffs, torn fabric on one sleeve, and a visor/goggle band in some head studies.

Bottom cinematic strip: Along the lower edge, include exactly 4 rectangular story panels: 1 younger character welding/cutting in space, 1 younger character close-up in helmet/cockpit, 1 older character repairing equipment with sparks, and 1 space exterior survival shot beside the rugged cracked-screen terminal. These panels should be darker and more cinematic than the main turnarounds, with star fields, spacecraft debris, cables, sparks, and a sense of zero gravity.

Constraints: No readable text of any kind, no logos, no extra characters, no cute chibi style, no bright clean sci-fi aesthetic. Keep the background flat dark charcoal and the sheet densely composed but readable.
```

She is captured at the decisive moment just before stopping, facing forward with a very slight natural angle, one leg stepping forward, relaxed shoulders and arms, and subtle motion in her hair and dress hem. Dress her in a luxurious {argument name="dress color" default="deep burgundy"} floor-length couture gown with an asymmetrical one-shoulder neckline, refined draping gathered at the waist, a tasteful front slit that allows natural walking movement, and rich satin fabric with soft highlights; pair it with slim matching strappy high heels. Place her fully inside the frame from head to toe, centered on a long red carpet with strong depth perspective. On both sides of the carpet, include crowds of photographers and spectators behind red velvet ropes with gold stanchions, camera flashes, and black evening attire. In the background, show a grand historic theater entrance or palace-like premiere venue, warm architectural lighting, overhead spotlights, and four tall vertical LED screens displaying abstract red-and-gold sparkling graphics, with two screens on each side of the walkway. Use cinematic event lighting, shallow background blur, crisp focus on the woman, natural body proportions, no exaggerated wide-angle distortion, no text, no watermark, no extra main characters, no cropped feet or dress hem. Use a {argument name="aspect ratio" default="1:1"} composition with enough surrounding space to show the red carpet depth and celebrity atmosphere.

```text
主題：
{argument name="主題" default="棚越しの視線"}

主体：
縦長2:3のリアルなライフスタイルポートレート。明るいナチュラルコスメショップの通路で、スマートフォンを両手に持つ成人女性が腰から軽く前へ身を傾け、振り返るようにカメラを見る。人物は画面中央やや右に配置し、頭部から太腿上部までを大きく写す。人物が画面の約65％を占め、左側には明るい通路の余白、右側には商品棚を長く見せる。

人物・表情：
自然で上品な大人の女性。肩に触れる長さのダークブラウンのボブヘア、軽い内巻きの毛先、薄いシースルーバング。ピンクベージュのアイメイク、細いアイライン、自然な涙袋、淡いコーラルリップ。頭を人物自身の左側へ少し傾け、顎をわずかに引き、柔らかな微笑みでレンズを見つめる。肌は明るくなめらかだが、毛穴や自然な陰影を残す。

服装・ポーズ：
{argument name="服装" default="アイボリーの細い肩紐付きリブキャミソールに、透かし編みのサマーカーディガンを肩からゆるく落として羽織る。淡いピーチ色の小花柄が入った、レース縁のティアードミニスカート。"}華奢なゴールドネックレスとブレスレット、ベージュのショルダーバッグ、バッグには小さな白いぬいぐるみチャーム。両手で白いスマートフォンを胸の下あたりに持ち、指先は画面へ自然に触れる。上半身を前へ傾け、腰を後方へ引き、両脚で安定して重心を支える。カーディガンの袖とスカートの裾に自然な重力と細かな布のしわを出す。

背景・光：
{argument name="背景" default="日本の都市部にある清潔感のあるナチュラルコスメショップ。"}画面右側の木製棚には、茶色いポンプボトル、白やベージュの化粧箱、小型の美容用品を整然と並める。値札やポスターの文字はぼかされて読めない状態。画面左奥にはガラス張りの入口と淡い緑の植栽が見え、通路の奥へ自然な遠近感が続く。主光は左側の入口から差し込む柔らかな昼の自然光。顔の左側、肩、鎖骨、腕に明るいハイライトを作り、人物の右側には薄い影を落とす。木製棚から暖かな反射光が返り、全体をアイボリーと淡いブラウンでまとめる。

構図・カメラ：
縦長2:3、目線よりわずかに高い位置から撮影したミディアムフルショット。人物の顔を画面上部中央、スマートフォンを左下寄り、バッグとぬいぐるみを右中央に配置する。上端には棚とポスターを少し残し、下端は太腿の途中で自然に裁切する。50mm相当、F1.8。人物の瞳と顔に正確にピントを合わせ、手元と衣服は鮮明、奥の棚と入口はなめらかにぼかす。人物の傾きと棚の水平線で、静かな動きのある構図にする。

質感・スタイル：
高精細なフォトリアル写真。自然な肌理、細い産毛、一本ずつ見える前髪、透かし編みの繊維、リブ素材とレースの細部を丁寧に描写。白飛びを抑えた明るい露出、低めのコントラスト、クリーミーなハイライト、淡いフィルム調の色彩。清潔感のある日本のファッション誌やコスメブランドのスナップ写真のような仕上がり。

ネガティブ：
不自然な顔、不自然な視線、過度に幼い印象、余分な指、欠けた指、融合した手、スマートフォンとの接触不良、歪んだ腕や脚、不自然な前傾姿勢、浮いた衣服、破綻したバッグの紐、誤った遠近法、光源と矛盾する影、読める商品名、文字化け、ロゴ、透かし、過度な美肌補正、プラスチックのような肌。
```

### 📷 例 4717：林间惬意肖像

![林间惬意肖像](../../images/%E6%9E%97%E9%97%B4%E6%83%AC%E6%84%8F%E8%82%96%E5%83%8F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
{argument name="人物" default="成年女性"}在{argument name="环境" default="遮阴林地"}环境中呈放松蹲姿。从头顶略上方至大腿上部紧凑取景。面部在左上中方；躯干斜向穿过画面中间；弯曲双腿在下三分之一形成大型曲线前景形状。身体四分之三朝向右侧；脸部转回朝向镜头。平静、略带沉思的表情——嘴唇轻微张开，眼神柔和专注。人物：纤细自然运动型体型，浅暖色调肌肤，精致面部比例，杏仁形深棕眼睛，柔和眉形，笔直精致鼻梁，低调玫红唇色。低调妆容：顺滑发光肌肤，柔和棕色眼线，自然睫毛，淡桃腮红，缎面质感裸玫瑰口红。真实毛孔，精细面部质感，自然眼下细节，轻柔色调过渡；无塑料感修图。发型：深棕黑色头发盘成宽松略显凌乱的低发髻于头部后方。自然发冠体积感，额头、太阳穴、耳朵与颈后有碎发丝；几缕看起来略有湿气的发锁垂落于面颊旁。随意且轻微蓬松——仿佛受潮湿或轻柔微风影响。细腻飞散发丝捕捉环境光。姿态与双手：左肘搭于画面左下部抬起的膝盖上。左手托住脸部与下颌侧——手指轻柔触碰面颊与太阳穴。右臂斜向向下横过画面中央，自然搭于对侧腿上。双肩放松，躯干略微前倾。准确人体解剖，真实关节，自然手指间距，与面部和腿部的真实接触感。泳装：{argument name="泳装" default="米色挂脖比基尼"}，不规则椭圆与圆形蓝色圆点图案。上装：细绕颈肩带，柔和收褶三角罩杯，胸部自然面料张力。哑光、轻盈、略有质感——真实接缝与褶皱。配套比基尼下装低腰于髋部，相同斑点图案。可见外侧髋部有小型银色金属环或圆形五金细节。不透明，合身，时尚写真感；非光亮或夸张。腿部：一条弯曲大腿显眼横过左下前景；另一只弯曲膝盖横过下中与右下。自然视角透视——最近的腿略大于躯干。真实膝盖轮廓，柔和肌肉线条，腰臀部自然肌肤褶皱，肩膀、手臂与大腿上有细腻高光。场景：茂密遮阴森林或热带花园。深绿色植被，虚化叶片，细枝条，藤蔓，模糊树干。几根浅色对角线树枝横过她的肩膀与头部背后——虚焦，用于框架而不分散注意力。深邃、有机、略带潮湿感。无建筑、小路、标识或人物。景深：极浅。眼睛/面部/上半躯干/最近手臂锐利清晰；植被与枝条化为柔和奶油焦外光斑。最近膝盖与画面下边缘略比面部柔和。背景：分层深橄榄绿、森林绿、炭灰、低调棕色。光线：来自头顶植被过滤的柔和自然日光，从左上方与左前方照射。轻柔照亮额头、鼻梁、嘴唇、颧骨、肩膀、锁骨、上臂与膝盖。略有露珠感的肌肤上有精致高光——尤其在右肩与上臂。下颌、发丝、手臂与躯干下方有柔和有细节的阴影。无刺眼直射阳光或闪光。色调与氛围：温暖米色、低调蓝色、柔和肌肤色、深森林绿、炭棕色、克制灰色。轻柔电影感色调：略冷的绿色阴影，中性高光，低至中等饱和度，顺滑高光过渡，精致对比。亲密、安静、感性但端庄、沉思感。相机：坐高平视，全画幅85mm，f/1.8–f/2.2。自然视角压缩，眼睛精确对焦，柔和背景分离，精致写真质感。轻微模拟胶片颗粒，轻柔边缘柔化，精致高光光晕。负面：无文字、无Logo、无水印、无多余人物、无强闪光、无变形肢体、无多余手指、无重复身体部位、无透明面料、无夸张比例、无过饱和色彩、无人工HDR、无塑料感肌肤。

### 📷 例 4720：日式庭院时尚人像

![日式庭院时尚人像](../../images/%E6%97%A5%E5%BC%8F%E5%BA%AD%E9%99%A2%E6%97%B6%E5%B0%9A%E4%BA%BA%E5%83%8F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
主題：
{argument name="主題" default="白布の結び目"}

主体：
{argument name="背景設定" default="緑豊かな日本庭園の池のほとりに立つ、成人女性を背後から捉えた縦長3:4のリアルなファッションポートレート。"}人物は画面中央やや右を大きく占め、頭頂から太腿付近までを収める。背中をカメラへ向けながら上半身を人物自身の右側へわずかにひねり、右肩越しに端正な横顔を見せている。

人物・表情：
自然で落ち着いた雰囲気の成人女性。髪は艶のあるダークブラウンの短いレイヤーボブで、長さは顎下から首筋まで。毛先は軽く外側へ流れ、薄い前髪と顔まわりの細い後れ毛が頬に沿う。淡いピンクブラウンのアイメイク、細く自然なまつ毛、柔らかな眉、控えめなローズベージュの唇。顔を斜め下へ向け、視線は画面右下へ静かに落とし、口元の力を抜いた物思いに沈む表情。

服装・ポーズ：
{argument name="服装" default="アイボリーホワイトの薄く柔らかなシフォンを重ねたロングドレス。"}肩から背中が大きく開いたデザインで、半透明の外布の内側には肌色に近い上品な不透明ライニングを設け、身体の輪郭をほのかに感じさせながら必要な部分は自然に覆う。左右の布を背中の中央、腰より少し上で厚みのある結び目にし、長い布端を下方へ流す。右腕は身体に沿って下ろし、右手で裾を軽くつまむ。左腕は布に包まれたまま脇へ下ろす。重心は左脚へ置き、肩の高さにわずかな差をつける。

背景・光：
画面左に穏やかな池と木々の反射、右に苔のついた自然石と古い木造建築の縁側。奥には障子戸、柱、深い緑の樹木を配置し、生活感を抑えた静かな日本庭園を構成する。左上から暖かな朝の光が差し込み、髪の輪郭、首筋、肩、背中の上部を柔らかく照らす。池からの青緑色の反射光を陰側へ薄く加え、木漏れ日の斑点を岩と縁側へ落とす。

構図・カメラ：
縦3:4。人物の顔を上部中央やや右、背中と布の結び目を画面中央に置く。左側には水面の明るい余白を広く取り、右側の縁側と石組みの斜線で奥行きを作る。カメラは人物の背後やや右、肩とほぼ同じ高さ。85mm相当の中望遠、浅い被写界深度。顔、髪の輪郭、肩、結び目に焦点を合わせ、池と建物は滑らかにぼかす。

質感・スタイル：
上品な日系ファッション写真。肌の細かなきめを残し、過度な美肌補正を避ける。シフォンの透過光、重なった布の濃淡、細かな皺、結び目の厚み、髪一本ごとの自然な艶を精密に描写。白と淡い肌色を中心に、背景の深い緑、木材の焦げ茶、水面の青緑を組み合わせる。柔らかなコントラスト、自然な立体感、静けさのある雑誌写真の仕上がり。

ネガティブ：
未成年に見える人物、過度な露出、露骨な裸、不自然な顔、不自然な視線、余分な指、欠けた指、手足の融合、関節の破綻、布と身体の接触不良、不自然な結び目、浮遊する布、左右の肩の破綻、誤った遠近法、光源と矛盾する影、過度な美肌補正、プラスチックのような肌、文字化け、ロゴ、透かし。
```

超写实生活方式时尚人像。活泼年轻成年{argument name="人物" default="东亚女性"}斜坐于{argument name="场景" default="复古公车、电车或铁路车厢"}内的{argument name="长椅颜色" default="深海军蓝色"}乙烯基长椅上。从头顶至延伸腿部的下方——最近的脚与小腿延伸至左下边缘之外。面部略偏右在画面中上方。弯起的膝盖在左中下方；另一条腿从短裤向左下前景对角线延伸——强烈纵深感，放松自发姿态。上半身略微前倾，头部轻柔向右肩倾斜，露出牙齿的明亮自然微笑直视镜头。温暖、友善、俏皮、亲和力强的表情——随性夏日出游感觉；非僵硬摆姿。人物：纤细优雅身形，温暖白皙肌肤，精致五官，柔和拱形深色眉毛，杏仁形棕色眼睛，精致鼻梁，自然珊瑚粉唇色。清新轻盈妆容：发光肌肤，淡桃腮红，柔和睫毛，细棕色眼线，中性眼影，自然亮泽唇色。真实毛孔，精细面部质感，轻柔高光，自然色调过渡；无过度修图。发型：极长浓密深棕黑色头发落至胸部以下，宽松略显蓬乱的波浪。柔和偏分，自然发冠体积感。大部分发丝垂落于双肩及上衣前方；几缕长发向外扫动环绕抬起的手。左手抬起头部旁侧的一缕发丝——手指轻轻穿梭于发丝间仿佛在玩弄。额头、面颊、发冠与肩膀周围有细腻飞散发丝被窗光柔和照亮。服装：精致白色短款上衣——轻盈半透明小圆点雪纺或精细孔眼面料。短款荷叶边袖子配柔和花边边缘；深V形领口配分层荷叶边装饰；胸下打结的前系带结构，两条细布料尾端垂向上腹部。面料上有低调散点微小灰蓝色圆点图案。露出适度腹部——精致且写真感。真实透明分层效果，缝制边缘，褶皱，收褶，柔和面料动感。高腰深靛蓝色牛仔短裤——腰部与髋部贴合，可见腰带环，金属扣，拉链门襟，弧形前口袋，铜棕色缝线，略浅牛仔色整洁卷边。真实牛仔纹理，接缝张力，凸起处褪色，细微髋部褶皱。白色低帮运动鞋部分可见于弯起腿下方的左中下边缘——圆形橡胶鞋底，白色鞋带，轻度磨损质感。腿部与手部：左膝在躯干左侧附近明显弯起，小腿向下折向椅子。右腿向镜头与左下角方向显眼延伸——轻微自然视角透视放大。人体解剖学准确的腿部：真实膝盖轮廓，细腻肌肤质感，轻柔高光。右手在右中下方髋部旁的椅垫上，手指自然弯曲支撑部分体重。一两只小型银色戒指配低调几何镶嵌。双手：正确手指数量，真实关节，短中性指甲，与发丝和座椅的真实接触感。长椅：深海军蓝色乙烯基——柔和光泽，略显磨损。真实褶皱，圆形垫子边缘，缝制接缝，细腻磨痕，宽阔反射高光。横向延伸至画面下半侧并向上升至软垫靠背。饱和蓝色与浅色上衣和温暖肌肤形成强烈对比。车厢内饰：老旧奶油色公共交通车厢。窗户被米白或浅米色漆面金属面板环绕——轻微变色，小划痕，可见螺丝、铆钉、铰链、接缝与略微风化边缘。最左侧：第二个窄小窗户的一部分，带圆角与粗金属框架。主窗顶部：水平拉丝金属导轨与滑动窗结构的机械配件。右下方窗框：嵌入式矩形金属把手与小型固定五金件。窗户与背景：主窗占据上方背景大部分，完全或部分开启。透过窗户：阳光明媚的公园，郁郁葱葱的绿色草地，叶片茂盛的落叶树，浅色步道。户外景观重度虚化——大型奶油绿色与黄色焦外光斑圆形。最亮日光从右上角射入——天空与树叶发光且略微过曝。窗外无可识别的建筑、标识、车辆或人物。光线：温暖晚上午或午后阳光从背后与右上方透过开窗照射——发丝右侧、肩膀、袖子、面颊与手臂上有发光金白色轮廓光。来自左侧的柔和正面补光保持面部均匀照明，眼睛保留自然反光点。额头、鼻梁、面颊、锁骨、腹部、大腿与光泽椅子上有精致高光。发丝、下颌、上衣褶皱与弯起膝盖下方有柔和开放阴影。明亮通透夏日曝光——窗户与发丝周围有轻柔逆光光晕。色调：奶油象牙、暖色肌肤、深海军蓝、深靛蓝、柔和绿色、浅黄色、拉丝银色、陈旧米色。自然略暖色彩；无过度饱和或橙色肌肤。相机：略微俯视坐高平视，全画幅50–70mm，f/2–f/2.8。眼睛/面部/上衣/双手/短裤/大腿上方锐利；窗框与户外植被逐渐虚化。真实视角，腿部轻微放大感。韩式写真夏日生活方式大片：青春、放松、发光、浪漫、自然、优雅。细腻模拟胶片颗粒，轻柔高光光晕，顺滑色调过渡，克制对比，高动态范围，细腻电影感色调。负面：无文字、无Logo、无水印、无多余人物、无品牌标志、无变形车厢几何、无变形手部、无多余手指、无重复肢体、无夸张体态、无过度透明服装、无塑料感肌肤、无强闪光、无浓重妆容、无人工HDR、无过饱和绿色、无窗外杂乱元素、无裁掉头部。

**Prompt:**

```text
主題： {argument name="テーマ" default="見下ろす朝"} 主体： 日本の都市型マンションのリビングで、両腕を頭の後ろに上げて立つ成人女性を、床に近い位置から見上げた縦長9:16のリアルなファッションポートレート。人物は画面中央を大きく占め、腰と太腿を手前に、顔と上半身を画面上部に配置する。脚は下端で自然に裁切し、人物全体に堂々とした成熟した存在感を持たせる。 人物・表情： {argument name="人物" default="落ち着いた大人っぽさのある女性"}。自然な丸みとメリハリを備えた健康的で成熟した体型。ダークブラウンのロングヘアに、薄めのシースルーバングと顔まわりの柔らかなレイヤー。毛先は軽く揺れ、作り込みすぎない日系ヘア。メイクはピンクブラウンのアイシャドウ、自然な涙袋、細いブラウンのアイライン、控えめなローズチーク、艶を抑えたピンクベージュのリップ。顎を少し引き、レンズを静かに見下ろす。眉と口元は力を抜き、落ち着いた自信のある表情。 服装・ポーズ： {argument name="トップス" default="白いリブ素材のクロップド丈ラップトップ"}。胸元は深すぎない自然なVネックで、前身頃が斜めに重なるデザイン。下は{argument name="ボトムス" default="ネイビーのサイドリボン付きスイムボトム"}。生地は透けず、腰骨を自然に包む上品なカット。人物自身の両腕を頭の後ろへ曲げ、肘を左右上方に開く。肩から脇、ウエストまで自然に伸ばし、骨盤をわずかに画面右へ傾ける。片脚に重心を置き、もう片方の膝を軽く緩める。トップスの伸び、裾の重なり、サイドリボンの結び目と垂れ方を重力に沿って正確に描写する。 背景・光： 明るいベージュと木目を基調とした日本のマンションのリビング。画面左に黒いサッシの大きな窓と白い薄手カーテン、前景左下にチャコールグレーのソファ。後景右には細い三脚型のフロアランプ、低い木製キャビネット、小さな観葉植物を配置する。窓の外にはぼかされた都市型集合住宅。左側の窓から柔らかな昼の自然光が入り、顔、胸元、腹部、左脚の輪郭を明るく照らす。右側には淡い陰影を残し、木製家具から弱い暖色の反射光が返る。天井はやや暗く、人物へ視線が集まる明暗構成。 構図・カメラ： 縦長9:16。カメラは床から40〜60cm程度の低い位置に置き、やや上向きに構える。24〜28mm相当の広角レンズ。腰と太腿を画面中央から下部に大きく配置し、顔を上部中央に収める。頭上には狭めの余白、左右には腕の輪郭が収まる程度の余白を残す。前景の脚を大きく見せながらも、身体の比率は自然に保つ。焦点は顔と上半身に合わせ、背景は軽くぼかす。スマートフォンの広角写真のような臨場感を持たせつつ、極端な歪みは避ける。 質感・スタイル： リアルな写真表現。自然な肌理、細かな産毛、腹部や脚の柔らかな陰影、リブ生地とスイム素材の違いを明確に描く。白、ネイビー、チャコールグレー、淡い木目を中心にした低彩度の配色。過度な美肌補正や人工的な光沢を避け、日常の室内で撮影された上質なファッションスナップの質感。輪郭はシャープすぎず、自然光による柔らかな立体感を重視する。 ネガティブ： 未成年、不自然な顔、不自然な視線、衣服の透け、余分な指、欠けた指、腕や脚の融合、関節の破綻、極端な広角歪み、不自然な体型、サイドリボンの浮遊、衣服との接触不良、不自然な重力、誤った遠近法、光源と矛盾する影、文字、ロゴ、透かし、過度な美肌補正、プラスチックのような肌。
```

人物：纤细优雅体型，温暖白皙肌肤，柔和肩线，自然体型比例。放松优雅的姿态——双臂收于背后，双手松散握于腰椎附近，大部分被遮挡。肩膀平直，脊背自然，低调的姿态。

侧面（右上方可见面部）：精致五官，笔直鼻梁，柔和眉形，低垂深色眼睛，低调玫红唇色。极简自然妆容：顺滑真实肌肤，淡腮红，轻描睫毛，柔和缎面唇色。自然毛孔，轻柔色调过渡；无人工美颜处理。

服装：合身短款吊带衫，低调{argument name="服装颜色" default="腮粉米色或浅尘土棕"}——极细意大利面肩带，从背面角度几乎不可见的直线领口，低方形背部。止于腰部上方，露出窄小的下背部区域。柔软顺滑略有弹性的面料，背部有淡水平褶皱与真实张力感。不透明，无Logo。高腰浅蓝色牛仔裤，贴合腰部、髋部与大腿上方——浅石洗质感，金米色对比缝线，中央后缝，腰带环，两只棱角分明的后口袋。两只后口袋上边缘有独特装饰细节：细腻白色蕾丝边配合浅粉色缎面蝴蝶结。几根细腻粉色丝带尾端垂直悬挂于口袋旁，不对称且被重力轻柔弯曲。装饰缝制在牛仔布上，有可见纺织质感与真实的缝制固定点。真实褪色感，轻微磨痕，接缝张力，轻微磨损的高光感。上腿部向画面下方延伸至裁切边缘。

汽车：从侧面看的光亮黑色轿车——后排乘客车门填满中央与下方背景。深色贴膜车窗，铬金属装饰，弧形车顶线，光亮黑色车漆。车身表面作为镜面：反射女性轮廓、周围树木、浅色天空与街道部分。车门与车窗上有分层反光——略比主体深且柔和。无品牌标志，无可辨认车牌。车窗斜向横过肩膀与上半躯干背后——铬金属边框作为深色玻璃与车顶之间的明亮窄线。车门下方近乎黑色，带细腻蓝灰高光与真实面板接缝。反光沿车身弯曲几何，而非平面。

背景：高挑深色铸铁围栏——竖向栏杆，装饰性对角线元素，几何交叉。围栏后有茂密常青灌木与细叶——深绿黑色质感背景。最左侧：部分浅米色石材或混凝土建筑柱子，可见矩形面板缝隙。细深色电缆或树枝在墙旁松散延伸，增添环境真实感。

光线：柔和自然日光，明亮阴天或开放阴影。来自上方略偏左的漫射光——照亮上背部、肩膀、手臂与发丝。发光但不过曝的肌肤。发冠、肩线、吊带衫肩带与牛仔接缝上有轻柔高光。发丝、手臂、腰部、车门与围栏周围有柔和有细节的阴影。

色调：光亮黑色、浅蓝色牛仔、腮粉米色、柔和粉色丝带、温暖白皙肌肤、深绿植被、低调石材、冷色铬金属。真实饱和度——粉色口袋点缀作为主要装饰色彩细节。无橙色肌肤、无过鲜艳蓝色、无人工HDR。

相机：胸至腰部高度，全画幅70–85mm，f/2.8–f/4。女性、服装细节与最近车面反光锐利清晰；铸铁围栏与植被略微柔化。自然视角压缩，准确体型比例。自然、女性化、精致、柔和电影感街头时尚风格。

负面：无文字、无水印、无品牌Logo、无多余人物、无重复反光、无变形车辆几何、无变形手部、无多余肢体、无夸张体态、无透明服装、无强闪光、无塑料感肌肤、无过饱和色彩、无浓重魅力修图、无杂乱背景。
```

### 🏛️ 例 4728：热带花园瑜伽倒立

![热带花园瑜伽倒立](../../images/%E7%83%AD%E5%B8%A6%E8%8A%B1%E5%9B%AD%E7%91%9C%E4%BC%BD%E5%80%92%E7%AB%8B.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
主題：
{argument name="主題" default="ヨガポーズ"}

主体：
陽光に満ちた{argument name="庭のスタイル" default="南国風の庭"}で、{argument name="服装" default="クリーム色の水着"}を着た成人女性が芝生の上でハンドスタンドのヨガポーズを取る。人物は画面中央に大きく配置し、頭から指先までを下半分、長く伸ばした脚を上半分に通して、全身が縦方向に収まる構成。

人物・表情：
自然で健康的な体つきの成人女性。明るくなめらかな肌に、日差しによる柔らかなハイライトが入る。黒に近いダークブラウンの髪を無造作なお団子にまとめ、逆さになった重力に沿って細い後れ毛が頬と地面の方向へ垂れる。顔は斜め下を向き、視線は両手の間へ落とし、集中した穏やかな表情。

服装・ポーズ：
アイボリー色のフリル付きセパレート水着。細いストラップと小さなサイドリボンを備えた軽やかなデザイン。両手を肩幅より少し広く芝生につき、指を大きく開いて体重を支える。肘はまっすぐ伸ばし、肩から腰までを安定させる。一方の脚は画面右上へ長くまっすぐ伸ばしてつま先を整え、もう一方の脚は膝を深く曲げ、足先を背中側へ向けたスタッグレッグ。骨盤に自然なひねりを加え、柔軟性と躍動感のあるシルエット。

背景・光：
前景は細かな芝の質感が見える明るい芝生。中景には石畳が斜め奥へ続き、左右を大きな熱帯植物と低木が囲む。画面左には赤い花穂を数本配置し、深い緑の中のアクセントにする。後景はヤシの葉、太い樹木、暗い石壁で構成し、自然な奥行きを作る。画面右上から暖かな午前の太陽光が差し込み、脚、背中、腕の右側を明るく照らす。身体と腕の影は画面左下の芝生へ落ち、葉からの反射光が肌の影側を淡く持ち上げる。

構図・カメラ：
縦位置3:4。全身を収めたロングショットで、人物が画面の約70％を占める。カメラは腰よりやや低い位置からほぼ水平に撮影し、上へ伸びる脚を強調する。両手は画面下部、頭は中央より下、伸ばした足先は上端近くに置く。背景は軽くぼかしつつ植物の形を残し、人物の身体と手指に焦点を合わせる。自然な50mmレンズ相当、過度な広角変形なし。

質感・スタイル：
実写のライフスタイル写真。温かい自然光、繊細な肌理、芝や葉の細かな質感、控えめなフィルム粒子。緑とアイボリーを中心に、赤い花を小さな差し色として配置する。健康的で清潔感があり、スポーツウェア広告とリゾート写真の中間のような落ち着いた仕上がり。

ネガティブ：
未成年に見える人物、不自然な関節、曲がった腕、余分な手足、欠けた指、融合した指、左右の脚の混同、不自然な重心、浮遊、道具との接触不良、誤った遠近法、光源と矛盾する影、過度な美肌補正、プラスチックのような肌、文字、ロゴ、透かし。
```

```text

创作一张电影感竖构图肖像，描绘一位硬派日本黑色电影风格侦探，场景位于雨后湿滑的东京屋顶，时间为日落时分。主体为 {argument name="character description" default="一位粗犷的中年日本男子，湿漉漉的后梳黑发，留着胡茬，表情严肃疲惫，颈部和胸部有明显的纹身"}，以四分之三正面角度站立，带着坚毅、沉思的神情微微俯视观众。他穿着 {argument name="outfit" default="深色细条纹西装，黑色开领衬衫，仿皮深色大衣，系着腰带，穿着宽松的长裤"}；一只手插在口袋里，另一只手拿着香烟垂在大腿旁。环境是密集的东京城市天际线，点缀着发光的窗户、霓虹灯和工业风的屋顶细节，地面因雨水而湿润，反射出强烈的光影。画面中需包含 3 个醒目的标志：右侧是一个写有「東京探偵 事務所」的大型风化屋顶广告牌，左侧是一个写有「ホテル ニュー東京」的红色垂直霓虹灯酒店招牌，背景中部是一个写有「ポンコ」的小型红色招牌。使用 {argument name="time and sky" default="从乌云中穿透而出的戏剧性金橙色日落"} 作为主要背光，在角色轮廓处形成边缘光，并在他的脸部和西装上营造出高对比度的阴影。风格应为超写实电影摄影，带有忧郁的新黑色犯罪剧质感，35mm 胶片感，浅景深，湿润的纹理，体积雾，丰富的琥珀色高光和深邃的黑色阴影，高细节皮肤纹理，逼真的布料褶皱，禁止卡通风格，禁止额外角色，禁止水印。

Create a cinematic vertical portrait of a gritty Japanese noir detective on a rain-slick rooftop in Tokyo at sunset. The subject is {argument name="character description" default="a rugged middle-aged Japanese man with wet swept-back black hair, stubble, a stern tired expression, visible neck and chest tattoos"}, standing three-quarter frontal and looking slightly down at the viewer with a tough, brooding attitude. ```text
[中文]
目标：创建一张并排对比图，展示两张 4:5 比例的竖版写实时尚大片肖像，中间由一条细白色垂直分割线隔开，模拟图像模型输出的对比效果。

画布：宽横向画布，包含 2 个完全相等的面板。每个面板均为透过玻璃拍摄的雨夜肖像，呈现冷蓝色城市灯光、深邃阴影、湿润倒影以及电影级的时尚杂志质感。

布局：左侧面板左上角贴有一个印有“A”的小型奶油色圆形徽章。右侧面板左上角贴有一个相同的奶油色圆形徽章，印有“B”。两个面板共同填满整个画面。

主体细节：每个面板展示同一位年轻女性，拥有自然的皮肤质感、湿润的深色头发、精致的妆容、略显疲惫的时尚表现力以及写实的身体结构。她面向镜头，双手持有一本奶油色的光面杂志。手指必须自然，每只可见的手均有五根手指。肖像透过布满雨滴的玻璃拍摄，因此前景中会出现雨滴和水迹。

面板 A：女性位于玻璃后方中心，光影略显深沉，湿发修饰脸部。她将杂志平举至胸前，正对镜头。底部包含一个反光的桌面或玻璃台面，映照出杂志和她的双手。背景中展示三个发光的蓝色几何霓虹符号：1 个圆形、1 个三角形和 1 个正方形。

面板 B：女性距离更近且角度略微倾斜，头部轻轻向观众左侧靠拢。她持有的杂志略微倾斜。背景更暗，带有蓝色城市灯光，远处同样有三个发光的几何符号：1 个圆形、1 个三角形和 1 个正方形。

杂志封面：杂志为奶油色光面质感，配有优雅的黑色衬线字体。主标题文字：{argument name="headline text" default="LOOK CLOSER."}，位于顶部，大号大写字母。下方为较小的副标题：{argument name="subtitle text" default="ONE PROMPT. ZERO FIXES."}。封面艺术展示一个透明的玻璃国际象棋骑士雕塑，旁边或穿过一朵红玫瑰。底部文字为：{argument name="date and issue" default="JULY 2026 · ISSUE 03"}。两张面板中的杂志设计需保持一致。

视觉风格：写实时尚大片、窗户雨滴纹理、光面高光、自然的皮肤毛孔、湿润的发丝、浅景深、蓝色霓虹光斑、柔和的奶油色调、高对比度夜景摄影、电影级写实感。

约束条件：整张图像必须包含 2 个肖像面板、2 个圆形面板标签、2 本杂志、2 位女性，以及总计 6 个霓虹几何符号：2 个圆形、2 个三角形和 2 个正方形。确保所有可见文字清晰且拼写正确。避免出现手指畸形、多余手指、字体扭曲或额外的标志。

[English]
Goal: Create a side-by-side comparison image showing two vertical 4:5 photorealistic fashion-editorial portraits at night, separated by a thin white vertical divider, as if comparing image model outputs.

Canvas: Wide horizontal canvas containing exactly 2 equal panels. Each panel is a rain-soaked nighttime portrait shot through glass, with cool blue city lighting, deep shadows, wet reflections, and cinematic fashion-magazine realism.

Layout: Left panel is labeled with a small cream circular badge containing “A” in the upper-left corner. Right panel is labeled with a matching cream circular badge containing “B” in the upper-left corner. The two panels together fill the frame edge to edge.

Subject details: Each panel shows the same young woman with natural skin, wet dark hair, subtle makeup, tired editorial expression, and realistic anatomy. She faces the camera while holding a glossy cream magazine cover with both hands visible. Her fingers must be natural, with five fingers on each visible hand. The portrait is photographed through rain-speckled glass, so raindrops and streaks appear across the foreground.

Panel A: The woman is centered behind the glass, slightly darker and moodier, with wet hair framing her face. She holds the magazine straight toward the camera at chest height. Include a reflective tabletop or glass ledge at the bottom, mirroring the magazine and her hands. Behind her, show three glowing blue geometric neon symbols: exactly 1 circle, 1 triangle, and 1 square.

Panel B: The woman is closer and slightly angled, leaning her head gently toward the viewer’s left. She holds the same magazine slightly tilted. Background is darker with blue city lights and the same three glowing geometric symbols in the distance: exactly 1 circle, 1 triangle, and 1 square.

Magazine cover: The magazine is glossy cream with elegant black serif typography. Main headline text: {argument name="headline text" default="LOOK CLOSER."} Large uppercase at the top. Smaller subtitle below: {argument name="subtitle text" default="ONE PROMPT. ZERO FIXES."} Cover art shows a transparent glass chess knight sculpture with a single red rose running through or beside it. Bottom line reads: {argument name="date and issue" default="JULY 2026 · ISSUE 03"}. Use the same magazine design in both panels.

Visual style: Photorealistic fashion editorial, rain-on-window texture, glossy highlights, natural skin pores, wet hair strands, shallow depth of field, blue neon bokeh, muted cream magazine tones, high contrast night photography, cinematic realism.

Constraints: Show exactly 2 portrait panels, exactly 2 circular panel labels, exactly 2 magazines, exactly 2 women, and exactly 6 total neon geometric symbols across the full image: 2 circles, 2 triangles, and 2 squares. Keep all visible text crisp and correctly spelled. Avoid distorted hands, extra fingers, warped typography, or additional logos.

目标：为 {argument name="character name" default="一位年轻东亚女性"} 创建一张简洁的摄影级角色参考图，设计风格类似于模型身份识别表，旨在实现 AI 角色生成的一致性。

画布：采用 16:9 宽屏横向布局，背景为纯白色摄影棚背景，由细浅灰色网格线划分为多个矩形面板。使用柔和均匀的摄影棚灯光、真实的皮肤纹理、中性色彩分级、锐利的编辑摄影风格，且不含任何装饰性背景元素。

布局与数量：包含 12 个可见的角色视图/面板。面板 1 为占据左侧三分之一的大幅正面特写肖像。面板 2 和 3 为顶部居中的头部及肩部四分之三侧面肖像。面板 4 和 5 为中部居中的左右侧面头部肖像。面板 6、7 和 8 为右上角的全身站立视图：正面、侧面和背面。面板 9、10、11 和 12 构成底部一排面部表情肖像：中性、浅笑、露齿大笑以及惊讶的微张嘴表情。

可见标签：在相关网格区域的左上角附近添加小型黑色日文分区标签，使用 {argument name="section labels" default="正面, 斜め, 横顔, 全身, 表情"}。标签需保持极简、无衬线字体且不显眼。

主体细节：该女性留着长长的 {argument name="hair color" default="黑色"} 波浪卷发，中分，化着柔和的自然妆容，皮肤白皙，大大的深色眼睛，眉毛精致，小巧笔直的鼻子，以及柔和的玫瑰色嘴唇。表情沉稳优雅。确保所有 12 个面板中的脸型、发长、面部比例和造型保持高度一致。

服装与配饰：她穿着一件修身的 {argument name="top color" default="白色"} 长袖圆领针织上衣，搭配高腰深炭灰色阔腿裤，黑色尖头鞋，佩戴短款珍珠项链和小型珍珠耳环。在全身视图面板中，她姿态挺拔，双臂自然垂于身体两侧。

限制条件：外观需呈现为真实的摄影棚照片参考图，而非动漫或插画。避免出现额外人物、道具、标志、水印、浓重阴影、彩色背景或额外文字。全身背面视图需清晰展示长发垂落背部及裤装细节。

[English]
Goal: Create a clean photographic character reference sheet for {argument name="character name" default="a young East Asian woman"}, designed like a model identity sheet for consistent AI character generation.

Canvas: Wide horizontal 16:9 layout on a pure white studio background, divided by thin light-gray grid lines into multiple rectangular panels. Use soft even studio lighting, realistic skin texture, neutral color grading, sharp editorial photography, and no decorative background elements.

Layout and count: Include exactly 12 visible character views/panels. Panel 1 is a very large close-up frontal portrait occupying the entire left third of the sheet. Panels 2 and 3 are upper head-and-shoulders three-quarter angled portraits in the top center. Panels 4 and 5 are left and right side-profile head portraits in the middle center. Panels 6, 7, and 8 are full-body standing views on the top right: front view, side view, and back view. Panels 9, 10, 11, and 12 form a bottom row of facial-expression portraits: neutral, slight smile, broad smile showing teeth, and surprised parted-lips expression.

Visible labels: Add small black Japanese section labels near the top-left of relevant grid areas, using {argument name="section labels" default="正面, 斜め, 横顔, 全身, 表情"}. Keep the labels minimal, plain sans-serif, and unobtrusive.

Subject details: The woman has long {argument name="hair color" default="black"} wavy hair parted near the center, soft natural makeup, fair skin, large dark eyes, delicate eyebrows, a small straight nose, and muted rose lips. Her expression is calm and elegant. Maintain the same face, hair length, facial proportions, and styling consistently across all 12 panels.

Wardrobe and accessories: She wears a fitted {argument name="top color" default="white"} long-sleeve scoop-neck knit top, high-waisted wide-leg dark charcoal trousers, black pointed shoes, a short pearl necklace, and small pearl earrings. In the full-body panels her posture is upright with arms relaxed at her sides.

Constraints: Make it look like a real studio photo reference sheet, not anime or illustration. Avoid extra people, props, logos, watermarks, heavy shadows, colorful backgrounds, or additional text. Keep the full-body back view showing long hair falling down the back and the trousers clearly visible.
```

### 🎴 例 4739：高端运动服饰广告大片，搭配 3D 字体设计

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
超高端运动服饰广告大片，主角为 {argument name="athlete" default="世界级足球明星"}，捕捉其在奔跑中的动态瞬间，悬浮于简洁的单色渐变背景之上。运动员身后是巨大的 3D 挤压字体，拼写出 "{argument name="player name" default="[球员姓名]"}"，色彩与主题完美呼应。浓郁的彩色烟雾从主体后方爆发，以极具张力的体积感、水墨晕染质感和电影级能量环绕其身。运动员身着单色超大款卫衣、带有彩色滚边的半透明机能风衣长裤，搭配同系列高端运动鞋、白袜及配套运动装备，呈现出大胆的同色系时尚造型。摄影棚布光，配以清晰的轮廓光、柔和的主光及细腻的反光，高对比度，超写实皮肤质感，定格动作，动态姿态，奢华 Nike 风格编辑美学（无可见 Logo），居中构图，极简背景，充满活力的 {argument name="color palette" default="单色调（蓝色、红色、绿色、橙色等）"}，广告大片品质，85mm 镜头，超锐利对焦，HDR，8K，照片级真实感，获奖商业体育摄影，高端杂志封面，大师级作品。

[English]
Ultra-premium sportswear campaign featuring a {argument name="athlete" default="world-class football star"} captured mid-stride in a dynamic running pose, suspended in motion against a clean monochromatic gradient background. Behind the athlete, oversized extruded 3D typography spelling "{argument name="player name" default="[PLAYER NAME]"}" dominates the composition, perfectly color-matched to the theme. A massive explosion of dense colored smoke erupts from behind the subject, wrapping around the body with dramatic volumetric depth, ink-in-water textures, and cinematic energy. The athlete wears a monochrome oversized sweatshirt, translucent technical windbreaker pants with colored piping, matching premium sneakers, white socks, and coordinated sportswear, creating a bold tone-on-tone fashion look. Studio lighting with crisp rim lights, soft key light, subtle reflections, high contrast, hyper-realistic skin texture, frozen action, dynamic posture, luxury Nike-inspired editorial aesthetic (no visible logos), centered composition, minimal background, vibrant {argument name="color palette" default="single-color palette (blue, red, green, orange, etc.)"}, advertising campaign quality, 85mm lens, ultra-sharp focus, HDR, 8K, photorealistic, award-winning commercial sports photography, premium magazine cover, masterpiece.

### 📷 例 4740：现代咖啡馆风格食品托盘摄影

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
明亮咖啡馆托盘布景
简洁生活方式食品摄影

使用 {argument name="reference source" default="上传的照片"} 作为主要视觉参考。

将图片中展示的 {argument name="subject" default="食品或饮料产品"} 转换为一张明亮、简洁的咖啡馆风格托盘摄影作品。

首先，视觉分析上传的图片，理解产品的真实外观、质感、颜色、份量、盛装方式及独特特征。

保留原始产品特征。

请勿重新设计配方、替换产品或将其变成通用的 AI 生成版本。

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
创意方向
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

创作一张明亮、简洁、现代的咖啡馆风格产品照片，采用托盘展示形式。

最终效果应呈现出：
- 明亮
- 简洁
- 清新
- 现代
- 写实
- 诱人
- 专业摄影感
- 商业化
- 适合社交媒体

这首先是一张食品摄影作品。

产品必须始终保持清晰的主角地位。

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
场景概念
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

营造一个精致的咖啡馆风格场景，将产品呈现在 {argument name="tray style" default="浅色木质托盘"} 或结构化的盛装底座上。

托盘应有助于组织构图，使布景显得整洁、刻意且高级。

整体场景应呈现出明亮的现代咖啡馆拍摄感。

偏好氛围：
- 明亮的早晨或日间咖啡馆
- 通透且简洁
- 简约而精致
- 视觉上宁静
- 高级但亲切

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
托盘与展示
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

使用托盘、项目或结构化的咖啡馆风格盛装底座作为关键构图元素。

如有需要，可以优化或替换原始的盘子、碗、杯子、纸垫、托盘或盛装容器。

选择一种感觉如下的布景：
- 简洁
- 现代
- 写实
- 高级
- 符合咖啡馆调性
- 视觉平衡

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
辅助元素
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

在适当的情况下，仅添加少量辅助元素。

可能的辅助元素：
- 一杯干净的水
- 简单的杯子或小型饮品搭配

- 小型餐具
- 小酱料碟
- 极简烘焙纸
- 微妙的食材暗示
- 若与产品自然契合，可添加少量配菜

保持所有辅助元素极简且处于次要地位。

请勿使托盘过于拥挤。

请勿营造杂乱的早餐铺陈。

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
背景与环境
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

营造一个明亮、简洁、视觉宁静的背景。

偏好视觉方向：
- 白色
- 柔和的灰白色
- 浅奶油色
- 浅米色
- 明亮的中性咖啡馆室内感
- 自然光下的干净桌面
- 柔和的极简背景虚化

根据上传的产品，自动选择最合适的明亮环境。

避免：
- 阴暗或忧郁的场景
- 杂乱的咖啡馆背景
- 厚重的乡村风格
- 强烈杂乱的图案
- 喧宾夺主的装饰道具

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
灯光
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

使用

[English]
BRIGHT CAFE TRAY SETUP
Clean Lifestyle Food Photography

Use the {argument name="reference source" default="uploaded photo"} as the primary visual reference.

Transform the exact {argument name="subject" default="food or beverage product"} shown in the image into a bright, clean, cafe-style tray setup photograph.

First, visually analyze the uploaded image and understand the product’s real appearance, textures, colors, portion, serving style, and distinctive characteristics.

Preserve the original product identity.

Do not redesign the recipe, replace the product, or turn it into a generic AI-generated version.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
CREATIVE DIRECTION
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Create a bright, clean, modern cafe-style product photo with a tray-based presentation.

The final result should feel:
- bright
- clean
- fresh
- modern
- realistic
- appetizing
- professionally photographed
- commercial
- social-media ready

This is a food photography visual first.

The product must remain the clear hero.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
SCENE CONCEPT
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Create a refined cafe-style scene where the product is presented on a {argument name="tray style" default="light wood tray"} or structured serving base.

The tray should help organize the composition and make the setup feel neat, intentional, and premium.

The overall scene should feel like a bright modern cafe photoshoot.

Preferred vibe:
- bright morning or daytime cafe
- airy and clean
- simple but polished
- visually calm
- premium but approachable

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
TRAY & PRESENTATION
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Use a tray, serving board, or structured cafe-style serving base as a key composition element.

You may improve or replace the original plate, bowl, cup, paper liner, tray, or serving vessel if needed.

Choose a setup that feels:
- clean
- modern
- realistic
- premium
- cafe-appropriate
- visually balanced

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
SUPPORTING ELEMENTS
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Add only a few subtle supporting elements when appropriate.

Possible supporting elements:
- a clean glass of water
- simple cup or small drink pairing
- napkin
- small cutlery
- tiny sauce dish
- minimal bakery paper
- subtle ingredient cue
- small side item if it naturally fits the product

Keep all supporting elements minimal and secondary.

Do not overcrowd the tray.

Do not create a busy breakfast spread.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
BACKGROUND & ENVIRONMENT
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Create a bright, clean, visually calm background.

Preferred visual direction:
- white
- soft off-white
- light cream
- pale beige
- bright neutral cafe interior feel
- clean table near natural light
- soft minimal background blur

Choose the most suitable bright environment automatically based on the uploaded product.

Avoid:
- dark or moody scene
- cluttered cafe background
- heavy rustic styling
- strong busy patterns
- loud decorative props

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
LIGHTING
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Us

### 📊 例 4741：瑞利散射信息图

![瑞利散射信息图](../../images/%E7%91%9E%E5%88%A9%E6%95%A3%E5%B0%84%E4%BF%A1%E6%81%AF%E5%9B%BE.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：制作一张长版单页教育科学信息图，解释 {argument name="headline text" default="为什么天空是蓝色的 —— 以及为什么日落是红色的"}，副标题为 {argument name="subtitle text" default="一页纸读懂瑞利散射"}。采用简洁的扁平化科学杂志设计风格，适合作为课堂讲义。

画布：垂直 9:16 海报，米白色背景，顶部和底部边缘由黑色横条框定。在每个黑色横条内居中放置白色文字“GPT Image 2”。主要信息图区域留有宽裕的页边距，配有细海军蓝分割线、圆角卡片，以及海军蓝/青色/珊瑚色/黄色的配色方案。

布局：使用 5 个编号部分，每个部分左侧标有深海军蓝圆形徽章，内含白色数字 1 到 5。所有字体采用几何无衬线体，标题为粗体大写海军蓝，副标题为青色斜体。

第 1 部分 — 可见光谱：标题为“可见光谱”。展示一条长圆角水平彩虹渐变条，从左侧的紫/蓝色延伸至右侧的青、绿、黄、橙和红色。下方添加 4 个波长标签：“400 nm”、“500 nm”、“600 nm”和“700 nm”，并带有小刻度线。

第 2 部分 — 阳光进入大气层：标题为“阳光进入大气层”。左侧绘制一个大型风格化黄色太阳，右侧为地球弧形边缘，中间是淡蓝色大气带，填充许多蓝色空气分子小点。展示 4 条蓝色水平光线进入并伴随蓝色小箭头发生散射，以及 3 条红色水平光线直接穿过射向地球。在大气层中包含 6 个较大的分子/恒星散射图标。在光线下方添加两个解释性说明：“蓝光：散射程度高约 5.5 倍”（蓝色）和“红光：直接穿过”（红色）。

第 3 部分 — 公式与柱状图：并排创建两个圆角矩形卡片。左侧卡片显示大公式“I ∝ 1/λ⁴”及说明“散射强度与波长的四次方成反比”。右侧卡片标题为“相对散射（450 nm 对比 650 nm）”。包含一个简单的柱状图，共有 2 根柱子：一根标有“450 nm”的高蓝色柱子，和一根标有“650 nm”的较短红橙色柱子；Y 轴标记为“相对散射”，右侧有大字“≈ 4.4 : 1”。

第 4 部分 — 正午与日落：并排制作两个大型圆形/椭圆形图表。左侧图表标题：“正午 —— 穿过大气层的路径较短”。显示弯曲地球地平线上方的小太阳、地面上的黑色小人剪影、天空中的蓝色点，以及穿过较短大气路径并带有蓝色散射箭头的垂直红蓝光线。右侧图表标题：“日落 —— 路径较长”。显示左侧靠近地平线的低位太阳、穿过弯曲地球上方淡蓝色大气层的长水平路径、沿途散射掉的蓝光、射向右侧黑色小人剪影的红光，以及说明“蓝光在到达你的眼睛之前已被散射掉”。

第 5 部分 — 事实：横向排列 3 张圆角事实卡片。卡片 1 带有简单的火星轮廓图标，文字为“事实：火星的日落是蓝色的”。卡片 2 带有青色海浪图标，文字为“事实：海洋呈现蓝色是出于不同的原因”。卡片 3 带有紫色眼睛图标及光线，文字为“事实：紫光散射最强，但我们的眼睛更偏爱蓝色”。

页脚：添加居中的小型来源文字：“来源：Rayleigh (1871) · 课堂用插图 · 图 1/1”。

视觉风格：扁平矢量插画，细海军蓝轮廓，仅在必要处使用柔和渐变，标签清晰易读，具备科学教科书的严谨性，除地球纹理可略微精细外，不采用写实风格。保持间距平衡，确保每个标签清晰可见。

[English]
Goal: Create a tall single-page educational science infographic explaining {argument name="headline text" default="WHY THE SKY IS BLUE — AND SUNSETS ARE RED"} with the subtitle {argument name="subtitle text" default="Rayleigh scattering explained in one page"}. Use a clean flat-design science-magazine look, suitable for a classroom handout.

Canvas: Vertical 9:16 poster on an off-white background, framed by black horizontal bars at the very top and bottom. Center the white text “GPT Image 2” inside each black bar. Main infographic area has generous margins, thin navy divider lines, rounded cards, and a navy/teal/coral/yellow palette.

Layout: Use exactly 5 numbered sections, each marked by a dark navy circular badge with white numbers 1 through 5 on the left. Keep all typography geometric sans-serif, with bold uppercase navy headings and teal italic subtitle.

Section 1 — Visible Light Spectrum: Heading reads “VISIBLE LIGHT SPECTRUM”. Show one long rounded horizontal rainbow gradient bar from violet/blue at left through cyan, green, yellow, orange, and red at right. Add exactly 4 wavelength labels beneath it: “400 nm”, “500 nm”, “600 nm”, and “700 nm”, with small tick marks.

Section 2 — Sunlight Entering the Atmosphere: Heading reads “SUNLIGHT ENTERING THE ATMOSPHERE”. Draw a large stylized yellow sun on the left, Earth’s curved edge on the right, and a pale blue atmospheric band between them filled with many small blue air-molecule dots. Show exactly 4 blue horizontal light rays entering and scattering with small blue arrows, plus exactly 3 red horizontal rays passing straight through toward Earth. Include exactly 6 larger molecule/star scattering icons in the atmosphere. Add two explanatory captions below the rays: “Blue light: scattered ~5.5x more” in blue and “Red light: passes through” in red.

Section 3 — Formula and Bar Chart: Create two rounded rectangular cards side by side. Left card shows the large equation “I ∝ 1/λ⁴” and the caption “Scattering intensity is inversely proportional to the fourth power of wavelength”. Right card title reads “RELATIVE SCATTERING (450 nm vs 650 nm)”. Include a simple bar chart with exactly 2 bars: a tall blue bar labeled “450 nm” and a shorter red-orange bar labeled “650 nm”, y-axis labeled “Relative scattering”, and large text “≈ 4.4 : 1” on the right.

Section 4 — Noon vs Sunset: Make two large circular/oval diagrams side by side. Left diagram title: “NOON — short path through atmosphere”. It shows a small sun above a curved Earth horizon, a tiny black human silhouette at ground level, blue dots in the sky, and vertical red and blue rays passing through a short atmospheric path with blue scattering arrows. Right diagram title: “SUNSET — long path”. It shows a low sun near the horizon on the left, a long horizontal path through a pale blue atmosphere over curved Earth, blue light scattering away along the path, a red ray continuing to a black human silhouette at the right, and the caption “Blue is scattered away before reaching your eye”.

Section 5 — Facts: Create exactly 3 rounded fact cards in a row. Card 1 has a simple outlined Mars-like planet icon and text “Fact: Mars sunsets are blue”. Card 2 has a teal ocean wave icon and text “Fact: The ocean is blue for a different reason”. Card 3 has a purple eye icon with rays and text “Fact: Violet scatters most, but our eyes favor blue”.

Footer: Add small centered source text: “Sources: Rayleigh (1871) · Illustration for classroom use · Fig. 1 of 1”.

Visual style: Flat vector illustration, thin navy outlines, soft gradients only where needed, crisp readable labels, science textbook clarity, no photorealism except the Earth texture may be lightly detailed. Keep spacing balanced and ensure every label is legible.
```

20 出头的东亚年轻女性。杏仁眼，双眼皮自然柔和，外眼角略微拉长。鼻梁挺直精致。肤色为白皙至浅米色（NC10–NC20），平滑均匀。在温暖的侧向吧台灯光下可见皮肤的次表面散射，鼻梁和颧骨上方有微小的镜面高光，额头上隐约可见细腻的粉底颗粒感。

温暖的琥珀色吊灯在她的背部和吧台表面洒下一层柔和的金光。远处招牌发出的冷蓝色霓虹光映照在背景的酒瓶上，营造出静谧的冷暖对比。氛围轻松从容——眼神柔和且失焦，沉浸在当下的舒适中，并未察觉到相机的存在。阴影区域带有细腻的 ISO 400 胶片颗粒感，呈现摄影噪点纹理，而非 CG 渲染的平滑感。宽高比 2:3。无水印，无文字覆盖，非卡通，非数字绘画，非插画，非动漫。

East Asian young woman in her early 20s. Almond-shaped eyes with a soft natural double eyelid, slightly elongated at the outer corners. Straight refined nose with a delicate bridge. Skin tone fair to light beige (NC10–NC20), smooth and even. Skin subsurface scattering visible under warm lateral bar light, specular micro-highlights on the nose ridge and upper cheekbone, fine foundation powder grain perceptible across the forehead.

She wears an {argument name="outfit" default="oversized off-white satin shirt tucked loosely into dark tailored trousers"} — effortlessly composed. She sits sideways on the stool, one arm resting along the bar counter, her torso mid-rotation, chin turning over her shoulder. Two or three stray hairs catch and scatter the light, displaced by faint air movement, natural unplanned imperfection, not geometrically symmetrical.

Warm amber pendant lights create a soft gold wash across her back and the counter surface. A cool blue-tinted neon glow from a distant sign touches the background bottles, creating a quiet warm-cool contrast. The mood is relaxed and unhurried — soft unfocused gaze, at ease in the moment, unaware of the camera. Subtle ISO 400 film grain in shadow areas, photographic noise texture not CG render smoothness. Aspect ratio 2:3. No watermark, no text overlay, not cartoon, not digitally painted, not illustration, not anime.

### 🎬 例 4743：雨中快递分镜故事

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：创作一个水彩分镜页面，讲述一个安静而神秘的投递故事，主角为 {argument name="character name" default="一位年长的女快递员"} 和她的狗。

画布：正方形图像，在白纸上呈现 3x3 分镜网格，带有细黑色间隙，采用柔和的水彩与墨水插画风格。在顶部和底部各添加一条黑色横带，中间均印有白色文字“Qwen Image 3.0”。

视觉风格：柔和的雨中城市水彩，柔和的灰色和绿色调，松散的墨水轮廓，轻柔的水洗背景，面板边缘略带暗角，具有电影感但呈现手绘质感。在所有相关面板中保持主角一致：一位 60 多岁的女性，留着银色短发，戴着红色圆框眼镜，穿着 {argument name="raincoat color" default="芥末黄"} 雨衣，戴着深色手套，穿着靴子，牵着一只系着蓝色牵引绳的小棕色腊肠犬。氛围安静、悬疑且忧郁。

布局：精确的 9 个面板，呈 3x3 网格排列。每个面板左上角有一个带有黑色轮廓和黑色数字的白色小圆圈，编号从 1 到 9。面板之间使用细黑色分割线。部分面板包含手写打字机字体的小标题框。

逐格内容，共 9 个面板：
1. 编号 1。黎明时分大雨中的广角镜头：老妇人站在一栋老式联排别墅的绿色前门处，伸手去按门铃或转动门把手，棕色腊肠犬站在潮湿的人行道上。背景是乔治亚风格的街道，有反光的积水和铁栏杆。标题框：“7:02 AM — 最后一次投递。”
2. 编号 2。特写镜头：她戴着手套的手拿着一个用红绳系着的棕色纸包裹。包裹标签上写着 {argument name="parcel label" default="Alder Lane 4B 单元"}。背景为雨中的灰色。
3. 编号 3。街景：妇人和腊肠犬在雨中的路边等待，背景中一辆城市公交车驶过，车灯倒映在湿漉漉的马路上。标题框：“城市从不等待。”
4. 编号 4。低角度仰拍老旧公寓楼的外立面：浅色石墙、高大的窗户、绿色门、铁栅栏，雨水落下。妇人从左下角抬头看向大楼，可以看到她的红色眼镜。
5. 编号 5。金属公寓门铃面板特写，清晰可见六个按钮，分别标有 1A、4B、2A、3B、4A，以及一个部分可见的下方按钮。一只沾满泥土的手套手指正按下 4B 按钮。
6. 编号 6。黑暗狭窄的门缝打开：一个年轻男人的脸从门内缝隙中窥视，半张脸隐没在阴影中，表情焦虑。标题框：{argument name="whisper caption" default="“你迟到了，”他低声说道。"}
7. 编号 7。特写镜头：包裹在黑暗的雨中门口被递出，红绳系成整洁的蝴蝶结，背景中有雨水划过的痕迹。
8. 编号 8。背影镜头：年长的妇人牵着系着蓝色牵引绳的腊肠犬，沿着雨中的街道走远。空旷的城市人行道，反光的地面，安静的联排别墅和树木逐渐消失在薄雾中。
9. 编号 9。最终定格镜头：从外部拍摄同一扇绿色联排别墅门，门现已关闭，红绳挂在门上或系在门附近。潮湿的人行道和栏杆旁稀疏的植物。标题框：{argument name="final caption" default="但她其实什么也没投递。"}

约束：在所有面板中保持主角形象一致。使用精确的 9 个编号面板和指定的标题。不要添加额外的对话气泡、额外角色或额外面板。保持神秘的雨中清晨氛围和手绘分镜外观。

Canvas: Square image with a 3x3 storyboard grid on white paper, thin black gutters, soft watercolor-and-ink illustration. Add a black horizontal band at the top and bottom, each with centered white text reading “Qwen Image 3.0”.

Visual style: Gentle rainy-city watercolor, muted grays and greens, loose ink outlines, softly washed backgrounds, slightly vignetted panel edges, cinematic but hand-painted. Keep the same protagonist in every relevant panel: a woman in her 60s with short silver hair, round red glasses, a {argument name="raincoat color" default="mustard yellow"} raincoat, dark gloves, boots, and a small brown dachshund on a blue leash. The mood is quiet, suspenseful, and melancholic.

Layout: Exactly 9 panels in a 3 by 3 grid. Each panel has a small white circle with a black outline and black number at the top-left corner, numbered 1 through 9. Use thin black dividers between panels. Some panels include small caption boxes in a handwritten typewriter-like font.

Panel-by-panel content, exactly 9 panels:
1. Numbered 1. Wide view at dawn in heavy rain: the elderly woman stands at the green front door of an old townhouse, reaching toward the doorbell or knob, with her brown dachshund beside her on the wet pavement. Georgian-style street in the background, reflective puddles, iron railings. Caption box: “7:02 AM — The last delivery.”
2. Numbered 2. Close-up of her gloved hands holding a brown paper parcel tied with red string. The parcel label reads {argument name="parcel label" default="Unit 4B, Alder Lane"}. Rainy gray background.
3. Numbered 3. Street scene: the woman and dachshund wait near a curb in rain while a city bus passes in the background, headlights reflected in the wet road. Caption box: “The city never waits.”
4. Numbered 4. Low-angle view of the old apartment building facade: pale stone walls, tall windows, green door, iron fence, rain falling. The woman looks up at the building from the lower left, her red glasses visible.
5. Numbered 5. Close-up of a metal apartment buzzer panel with exactly six visible buttons labeled 1A, 4B, 2A, 3B, 4A, and one partially visible lower button. A muddy gloved finger presses the 4B button.
6. Numbered 6. Dark, narrow door opening: a young man’s face peers through the crack from inside, half hidden in shadow, anxious expression. Caption box: {argument name="whisper caption" default="You’re late,” he whispered."}
7. Numbered 7. Close-up of the parcel being handed forward against a dark rainy doorway, red string tied in a neat bow, water streaks in the background.
8. Numbered 8. Rear view of the elderly woman walking away down the rainy street with her dachshund on the blue leash. Empty city sidewalk, reflective pavement, quiet townhouses and trees fading into mist.
9. Numbered 9. Final static shot of the same green townhouse door from outside, now closed, with the red string hanging from or tied near the door. Wet pavement and sparse plants by the railings. Caption box: {argument name="final caption" default="But she never delivered anything at all."}

Constraints: Keep the protagonist consistent across all panels. Use exactly 9 numbered panels and exactly the captions specified. Do not add extra speech bubbles, extra characters, or extra panels. Maintain a mysterious rainy morning atmosphere and a hand-painted storyboard look.

页脚：添加一条深蓝色的狭窄页脚条，左侧带有微小的港口图标，中间以奶油色文字显示“{argument name="footer details" default="Pier 7, Harbor District — Free entry — harborlights.example.com"}”，右侧带有小锚图标。

视觉风格：复古日式旅游海报，丝网印刷质感，采用焦橙色、赭奶油色、深蓝色、青色、梅紫色和黑色的有限调色板，套色不准效果，磨损边缘，醒目的海报排版，怀旧的夏夜氛围。

约束：保持所有文字清晰可辨，拼写与指定内容完全一致。必须使用 1 条竖向书法横幅、3 张底部特色卡片和 2 艘大型前景船只。禁止使用现代 UI 元素、禁止写实风格、禁止添加额外的标志或水印。

Canvas: Tall vertical poster, warm sunset-to-night palette, deep orange at the horizon fading into plum purple and dark indigo at the top, with heavy grainy risograph texture, aged paper speckles, slightly distressed ink, and cream-colored borders/details.

Layout: At the top, show a small crescent moon on the left and a large bold hand-painted brush headline centered across three lines reading “HARBOR / LIGHTS / NIGHT MARKET” in rough cream lettering. On the upper right edge, add one narrow vertical cream banner with black Japanese calligraphy reading 「夏の思い出をここで」. Beneath the title, add a smaller Japanese subtitle “ハーバーライツ夜市” and an italic Spanish subtitle “Mercado Nocturno Luces del Puerto”. Below that, place a rounded rectangular date plaque with a cream fill and red outline reading “{argument name="date and time" default="SAT · AUG 15 · 5PM–MIDNIGHT"}” in tall navy condensed type.

Main illustration: Fill the middle with a flat illustrated harbor night market scene. Include a dark navy skyline with many small yellow-lit windows, distant purple mountains, a tower resembling a harbor observation tower near the center, water with orange and gold reflections, and multiple strings of glowing paper lanterns hanging between poles and boats. Show exactly 2 large foreground boats: one on the lower left with a vertical sign reading 「夜市」 and silhouetted people eating or shopping, and one on the lower right with seated silhouettes under lanterns. Add smaller boats in the middle distance, cranes or mast-like poles, and scattered stylized clouds.

Bottom feature cards: Along the bottom, create exactly 3 rounded rectangular illustrated cards with cream borders. Card 1 shows a steaming ramen bowl with egg, seaweed, noodles, and greens, labeled “STREET FOOD / 屋台グルメ”. Card 2 shows a black vinyl record with orange center and four small music-note icons around it, labeled “LIVE MUSIC / 音楽ライブ”. Card 3 shows a handmade ceramic cup or vase with blue glaze drips, labeled “LOCAL CRAFT / 手作り市”.

Footer: Add a narrow dark navy footer strip with tiny harbor icons on the left, the line “{argument name="footer details" default="Pier 7, Harbor District — Free entry — harborlights.example.com"}” centered in cream, and a small anchor icon on the right.

Visual style: Vintage Japanese tourism poster, screen-printed texture, limited color palette of burnt orange, ochre cream, navy, teal, plum, and black, imperfect registration, distressed edges, bold poster typography, nostalgic summer night atmosphere.

Constraints: Keep all text legible and spelled exactly as specified. Use exactly 1 vertical calligraphy banner, exactly 3 bottom feature cards, and exactly 2 large foreground boats. No modern UI elements, no photorealism, no extra logos or watermarks.

```text
[中文]
目标：创建一个逼真的 ChatGPT 移动端 App 垂直手机截图，展示图像生成结果，采用简洁的 iOS 界面。

画布：9:16 比例的高长手机截图，白色背景，圆角 iPhone 安全区域边缘，底部带有黑色 iOS 主屏幕指示条。顶部状态栏左侧显示 9:33，右侧显示 4 个状态图标：蜂窝信号栏、Wi-Fi 图标、51% 的电池百分比以及电池形状。

布局：顶部导航栏包含 3 个控件：左侧的汉堡菜单图标、右上角附近的编辑/撰写图标，以及最右侧的三个点更多菜单。下方放置一个略微靠右的浅灰色圆角用户消息气泡。气泡下方添加一个写有“思考”的小型灰色标签。标签下方展示一张带有圆角的大型生成照片卡片，接着是一行包含 8 个小型缩略图的水平条。底部显示一个带有加号图标、占位符文本、麦克风图标和蓝色圆形语音按钮的圆角输入栏。

文本内容：用户消息气泡包含中文文本：“{argument name="prompt text" default="Masochist系男友视角斜侧手持手机拍摄风格，风格清新干净，一种随性松弛氛围感，随手记录。绝美，表情随机；ar9:16\n\n连续10张单独图"}”。底部输入框占位符显示为“询问 ChatGPT”。

主照片主体：大型生成图像是一张 {argument name="character description" default="一位留着长黑发的年轻东亚女性"} 的随手抓拍人像，她坐在室内的绿色软垫椅子上，对着镜头温暖地微笑。她穿着 {argument name="outfit" default="米色罗纹背心和宽松的白色长裤"}。姿势显得放松且自然，采用略微倾斜的男友视角手机拍摄角度。她的双手随意放在膝盖附近，表情明亮自然，光线为柔和的日间窗光。

照片内环境：舒适的公寓客厅/餐厅，铺设深色木地板，配有透明白纱帘和深色侧帘，窗外可见绿植，背景中有一张配有高背深色椅子的餐桌。保持照片的真实感，略带不完美和随性，而非影棚级的精致感。

叠加控件：在大照片卡片底部附近包含 2 个半透明圆形叠加按钮：左侧按钮标注为“编辑”，右侧按钮带有上传/分享箭头图标。缩略图条包含 8 个小型圆角预览图，展示同一女性的其他相似人像；最后一个缩略图处于选中状态，带有细微的浅色轮廓。

视觉风格：照片级真实的 iOS 截图，简洁的白色边距，柔和的灰色界面元素，准确的移动端 App 间距，自然的中文 UI 排版，无额外水印，无额外面板，除 1 张主图和 8 张缩略图外，不包含其他生成的图像。

[English]
Goal: Create a realistic vertical smartphone screenshot of the ChatGPT mobile app showing an image-generation result, in a clean iOS interface.

Canvas: Tall 9:16 phone screenshot, white background, rounded iPhone safe-area edges, black iOS home indicator at the bottom. Status bar at the top shows 9:33 on the left and exactly 4 right-side status items: cellular signal bars, Wi-Fi icon, battery percentage reading 51, and a battery shape.

Layout: Top navigation row contains exactly 3 controls: a hamburger menu icon on the left, a small compose/edit icon near the upper right, and a three-dot more menu at the far right. Below it, place one light-gray rounded user message bubble aligned slightly right. Under the bubble, add a small gray section label reading “思考”. Below the label, show one large generated photo card with rounded corners, then a horizontal strip of exactly 8 small thumbnail images. At the bottom, show one rounded input bar with a plus icon, placeholder text, microphone icon, and a blue circular voice button.

Text content: The user message bubble contains Chinese text: “{argument name="prompt text" default="Masochist系男友视角斜侧手持手机拍摄风格，风格清新干净，一种随性松弛氛围感，随手记录。绝美，表情随机；ar9:16\n\n连续10张单独图"}”. The bottom input placeholder reads “询问 ChatGPT”.

Main photo subject: The large generated image is a casual handheld portrait of {argument name="character description" default="a young East Asian woman with long dark hair"} sitting indoors on a green upholstered chair, smiling warmly at the camera. She wears {argument name="outfit" default="a beige ribbed tank top and loose white pants"}. The pose feels relaxed and candid, shot from a slightly high, tilted boyfriend-perspective phone angle. Her hands rest casually near her lap, her expression is bright and natural, and the lighting is soft daytime window light.

Environment inside the photo: A cozy apartment living/dining room with dark wood floor, sheer white curtains, darker side curtains, a window with greenery outside, and a dining table with tall dark chairs in the background. Keep the photo realistic, slightly imperfect, and spontaneous rather than studio-polished.

Overlay controls: On the large photo card, include exactly 2 translucent circular overlay buttons near the bottom: one left button labeled “编辑” and one right button with an upload/share arrow icon. The thumbnail strip contains exactly 8 small rounded previews showing alternate similar portraits of the same woman; the final thumbnail is selected with a subtle light outline.

Visual style: Photorealistic iOS screenshot, clean white margins, soft gray interface elements, accurate mobile app spacing, natural Chinese UI typography, no extra watermarks, no additional panels, no extra generated images beyond the 1 main image and 8 thumbnails.
```

```text
[中文]
创作一张超写实的竖构图生活照，使其看起来与在家中用 iPhone 随手抓拍的照片完全无异。保留核心场景结构：一位外表健康、充满运动感的欧裔女性，身材自然优美且匀称，正站在餐桌旁，将玻璃水瓶中的水倒入水杯。进一步强化真实感、自然感和“去 AI 化”的质感，让照片看起来更像是旁人随手捕捉的真实瞬间，而非精心设计的广告大片、生成的图像或构图完美的艺术品。女性必须看起来完全真实且未经修饰：健康、匀称、优雅且可信，手臂、腰部、肩膀和姿态带有自然的肌肉线条，但没有夸张的肌肉轮廓。面部应呈现真实的毛孔、细微的不对称感、自然的眼下纹路、真实的唇部质感、肤色的细微变化以及普通人的真实感。她应展现出一种安静、写实的美，像是一个拥有出色自然条件的真人，而非精修的模特。她的体型应健美、柔美且真实，拥有自然出众但依然极其真实的轮廓。保持餐桌场景的逻辑，但让整个瞬间显得更随意、更可信。她站姿放松而自然，身体微微前倾，手部握住水瓶和水杯的动作带有真实的张力。表情平静、内敛且未摆拍，仿佛专注于倒水，并未意识到正在被拍摄。头发简单扎起，带有真实的碎发和不规则的发丝，没有沙龙造型的完美感。服装保持简约、浅色调：一件修身的奶油色罗纹长袖上衣搭配同色系长裤或半身裙，呈现出真实的织物褶皱、轻微的拉伸感、自然的贴合度以及日常的柔软感。餐桌应显得更有生活气息，而非刻意摆拍。保留精美的物品，但以更真实、随意的形式摆放：柠檬、面包、透明与有色玻璃杯、陶瓷碗、瓶子、水果、切开的石榴、折叠的餐布以及日常生活中细小的杂乱感。不要有过度修饰的痕迹。物品间的间距应带有不完美的人文感，就像用餐中途被中断的真实场景。倒水的动作必须格外逼真：真实的玻璃折射、轻微的溢出风险感、准确的水流厚度、透过水瓶产生的细微畸变，以及接收水杯中真实的倒影。保留窗边或玻璃门旁的温暖室内环境，但进一步强化自然光的真实感。光线应像真实的阳光射入室内并随时间移动，而非专业的摄影布光。使用不均匀的阳光斑块、柔和的阴影过渡、明亮玻璃区域真实的过曝感、细微的曝光衰减、轻微的动态范围瑕疵，以及真实手机摄像头在混合光线下常有的冷暖色差。背景应包含深色反光玻璃、窗帘阴影、模糊的户外倒影、椅子和柔和的室内纵深感，所有细节均以真实的手机摄像头质感呈现。让照片更具抓拍感，减少构图痕迹。构图应显得略微随意：极细微的裁切张力、轻微的手持倾斜或非中心平衡，甚至是一点点距离上的不一致，使其看起来是真实拍摄而非精心设计。女性主体保持在场景中心，但让构图在不完美的人文感中留有呼吸空间。使用真实的 iPhone 风格成像特征：轻微的计算摄影景深柔化、真实的边缘锐度衰减、自然的溢光效果、普通的白平衡偏差、适度的局部对比度以及真实的纹理处理。通过细微的真实瑕疵进一步强化“去 AI”的写实感：桌上物品角度略显笨拙、玻璃上的细微指纹或水渍、腰部的轻微褶皱、自然的手指摆放位置、皮肤纹理的细微差异、指甲的不完美感、水果表皮的轻微不平整、非完全对称的构图，以及光线在不同表面上的真实漫反射。照片应像一段真实的记忆，而非一张“精美的渲染图”。色调保持低调自然：奶油色服装、暖色木纹、柔和的柠檬黄、柔和的石榴红、橄榄棕色玻璃瓶、透明水杯、亚麻米色和温暖的室内阴影。保持色彩真实且不刻意。不要过度调色，不要过度电影化，不要添加强烈的风格化滤镜。整张照片应呈现出自然且出色的手机摄影质感。可以保留细微的噪点，但真实感必须放在首位。细节仅在自然对焦处保持锐利，其余部分以真实的手机摄像头方式柔化。最终图像必须给人以亲密、居家、优雅、健康且绝对真实的感受，没有任何明显的 AI 痕迹。无文字、无 Logo、无品牌标识、无广告排版、无排版设计、无影棚质感、无人工对称、无塑料感皮肤、无夸张姿势、无奢侈品广告的僵硬感、无明显的后期修图、无过度完美的物品摆放。

[English]
Create a hyper-real vertical lifestyle photo that feels completely indistinguishable from a real casual iPhone snapshot taken at home, preserving the same core scene skeleton: a healthy athletic adult European-looking woman with a naturally beautiful, toned figure stands beside a dining table while pouring water from a glass carafe into a drinking glass. Push the realism, naturalness, and anti-AI quality further so the image feels even more like an unplanned real-life moment captured by someone nearby, not a styled campaign, not a generated image, not a perfect composition. The woman must look fully human and unretouched: healthy, fit, elegant, and believable, with subtle muscle tone in the arms, waist, shoulders, and posture, but without exaggerated definition. Her face should show realistic pores, tiny asymmetries, soft under-eye naturalness, real lip texture, slight variation in skin tone, and ordinary human presence. She should feel attractive in a quiet, realistic way, like a real person with excellent natural features rather than a glossy model. Her body shape should be strong, feminine, and authentic, with a naturally outstanding silhouette that still feels completely real. Keep the same domestic dining-table logic, but make the whole moment more spontaneous and more believable. She stands in a relaxed but natural posture, slightly leaning into the pouring action, with realistic hand tension around the carafe and glass. Her expression should be calm, inward, and unposed, as if she is focused only on the water and unaware of being photographed. Hair is tied back simply with believable flyaways, subtle irregular strands, and no salon perfection. Clothing remains soft, minimal, and light-toned: a fitted ribbed cream long-sleeve top and matching light pants or skirt, but with real fabric creases, slight pull tension, natural fit variation, and everyday softness. The dining table should feel more naturally lived-in and less styled. Keep beautiful objects, but arrange them with more believable randomness: lemons, bread, clear and tinted glasses, ceramic bowls, a bottle, fruit, cut pomegranate, folded cloth, and small everyday table disorder. Nothing should look over-curated. Object spacing should feel imperfect in a human way, like a real home meal setup interrupted mid-moment. The water pour must be especially realistic: true glass refraction, slight spill risk tension, accurate stream thickness, subtle distortion through the carafe, and believable reflections in the receiving glass. Preserve the warm interior setting near a window or glass door, but push the available-light realism further. The light should feel like actual sunlight entering a home and moving across the room, not professional lighting. Use uneven sun patches, soft shadow transitions, realistic highlight clipping in bright glass areas, subtle exposure roll-off, mild dynamic-range imperfection, and slight warm-cool imbalance that real phone cameras often produce in mixed light. The background should include dark reflective glass, curtain shadow, vague outdoor reflection, a chair, and softened interior depth, but all rendered with true-to-life phone-camera softness. Make the image more candid and less composed. The framing should feel slightly accidental: a very subtle crop tension, a tiny handheld tilt or off-center balance, maybe a mild closeness inconsistency that makes it feel genuinely photographed rather than perfectly designed. Keep the woman mostly central to the scene logic, but let the framing breathe in an imperfect, human way. Use realistic iPhone-style image behavior: slight computational depth softness, authentic edge sharpness falloff, natural highlight bloom, ordinary white balance imperfection, modest local contrast, and realistic texture handling. Push the anti-AI realism further through small truthful imperfections: a slightly awkward object angle on the table, tiny smudge or water mark on glass, subtle wrinkle at the waistline, ordinary finger placement, mild skin texture variation, imperfect nail appearance, slight unevenness in fruit skin, not-quite-symmetrical composition, and light behaving differently across surfaces. The image should feel like a real memory, not a “beautiful render.” Color palette should remain understated and natural: cream clothing, warm wood, muted lemon yellow, soft pomegranate red, olive-brown bottle glass, transparent drinking glass, linen beige, and warm interior shadow. Keep colors believable and unforced. Do not over-grade, do not cinematicize too much, do not add heavy stylization. The whole picture should feel like a naturally excellent phone photo. Subtle print grain can be present, but realism must come first. Fine detail should be sharp only where naturally focused and softened elsewhere in a believable phone-camera way. The final image must feel intimate, domestic, elegant, healthy, and absolutely real, with zero obvious AI traces. No readable text, no logo, no branding, no ad layout, no typography, no studio glamour, no artificial symmetry, no plastic skin, no exaggerated pose, no luxury-campaign stiffness, no obvious retouching, no overly perfect object placement.
```

### 🏷️ 例 4754：二值化可通行区域遮罩

![二值化可通行区域遮罩](../../images/%E4%BA%8C%E5%80%BC%E5%8C%96%E5%8F%AF%E9%80%9A%E8%A1%8C%E5%8C%BA%E5%9F%9F%E9%81%AE%E7%BD%A9.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
为俯视视角电子游戏地图创建一个纯黑白色的二值化可通行区域遮罩。使用 1:1 的正方形画布（约 768×768 像素），背景为纯黑色，中间包含一个单一连通的白色不规则区域，代表可通行地面。白色形状应类似于奇幻地下城或岛屿的轮廓：拥有宽阔的中心垂直开放区域、锯齿状的有机边缘，以及向左右两侧延伸的狭窄走廊和突出房间。在中心白色区域周围精确包含 6 个显著的侧面特征：左上方有一个通过细颈连接的大型圆形平台；左下方有一个通过蜿蜒走廊连接的较小蘑菇状平台；左上边缘有一个锯齿状的新月形突出部；右上角有一个不规则的房间，内部带有三个狭窄的黑色垂直切口；右侧中心边缘有一个大型黑色封闭团块状障碍物，带有弯曲的钩状尖端；右下方有一个圆形的房间状突出部，被黑色负空间部分隔开。添加若干小型三角形缺口和像素阶梯状的锯齿边缘，但必须严格保持图像仅有两种颜色：纯白色代表可通行空间，纯黑色代表不可通行空间。无文字、无灰度、无抗锯齿、无阴影、无透视、无纹理；边缘应略显像素化，呈现出遮罩效果，如同专为 SDF 或碰撞地图转换而设计。

[English]
Create a pure black-and-white binary walkable-area mask for a top-down video game map. Use a square 1:1 canvas, about 768×768 pixels, with a solid black background and a single connected irregular white region representing traversable ground. The white shape should resemble a fantasy dungeon/island silhouette: a broad central vertical open area, jagged organic edges, narrow corridors and protruding rooms branching left and right. Include exactly 6 prominent side features around the central white mass: 1 large rounded oval platform on the upper left connected by a thin neck, 1 smaller mushroom-like platform on the lower left connected by a winding corridor, 1 jagged crescent-shaped protrusion at the upper left edge, 1 irregular upper-right chamber with three narrow black vertical cutouts inside it, 1 large black enclosed blob-like obstacle intruding from the right-center edge with a curled hook tip, and 1 round lower-right room-like protrusion partially separated by black negative space. Add several small triangular notches and pixel-stepped jagged edges, but keep the image strictly two colors only: pure white for walkable space and pure black for non-walkable space. No text, no grayscale, no antialiasing, no shadows, no perspective, no texture; make the edges slightly pixelated and mask-like as if intended for SDF or collision-map conversion.
```

**来源：** [@じょにがたロボ](https://x.com/jonigata_ai/status/2079917182860116349) | 2026-07-22
